````json
  "id": "2026-01-20",
  "posted_at": "2026-01-20",
  "raw_content": "- Respawn time at 19 minutes reduced from 35s to 30s\n- Respawn time at 30 minutes reduced from 75s to 70s\n- You now have a longer respawn time if you die and are meaningfully richer than the enemy team's average net worth (scales from +6s to +22s penalty, based on networth being 15 to 30% higher and game time being 10min to 25min, if under 15% NW or 10min, nothing happens)\n\n- Neutral respawn times, hp, and bounty reduced by 30% (sinner's sacrifice unaffected)\n\n- Urn no longer requires a drop off channel time\n- Urn is now knocked out of your hands with a light melee (instead of only with a heavy melee)\n- Urn pickup channel time is reset if you get hit with a light or heavy melee\n- Can no longer parry while channeling to pickup the urn\n- Urn carrier resist reduced from 50% to 30%\n\n- Down Dash stamina cost reduced from 1 to 0.5\n- Stamina Regen in the healing zone is now dramatically increased\n- Kinetic Dash: Ammo count reduced from +8 to +6\n\n=== Patch 2 ===\n- Urn now drops if it travels through doorman portals",
  "title": "01-20-2026 Update",
  "url": "https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/12-29-2025-update.95233/post-185824"
}
````

Mid Boss now has 35% Debuff Resistance\n\n* Rejuv drop duration reduced from 7s to 6s\n\n* Rejuv duration no longer refreshes when hitting a crystal later\n\n\n* Minimap now indicates if the Urn is a favored, neutral or unfavored\n\n\n* Added a neutral camp (2 Normal, 3 Weak) at Hidden King Park Walker\n\n* Added a neutral camp (2 Normal, 3 Weak) at Archmother York Walker\n\n* Added \"0 Way\" veils underneath the Shrine platforms in each base\n\n* Moved the mid lane bridge above the Hidden King Base entrance more to the middle of the two edges\n\n\n* Subsequent CC reduction increased from 8%/24% to 10/30% (window from 7s to 8s)\n\n* Move speed diminishing ramp now occurs around 11m rather than 10m\n\n* Fixed melee abilities not using conditional damage based on enemy state (close quarters, point blank, opening rounds)\n\n\n* Street Brawl: Removed item slot limit (don't need to sell items in the final round sometimes)\n\n\n\n\n[ Items ]\n\n\n* Added new T1 Spirit Item: Golden Goose Egg\n\n\n* Extended Magazine: Weapon Damage increased from +6% to +8%\n\n* High-Velocity Rounds: No longer grants +5% Fire Rate\n\n* High-Velocity Rounds: Now grants +8% Weapon Damage\n\n* Lifesteal provided by things like Soul Shredder bullets now properly diminishes in stacking with other sources of lifesteal\n- Fix an edge case where a player can claim the Rejuv but also get parried\n- Fixed spellslinger headshots providing more bonuses than intended\n- Fixed some misc mispredictions during Ice Path movement\n- Fixed Ice Path being overly sticky for allies\n\n- Fixed Ivy,   "raw_content": "[ General ]\n\n- Soul Orbs no longer have special duplication rules before 8 minutes. Soul splitting rules are now the same before 8 minutes as afterwards. There is also no longer a bounty bump that occurs at 8 minutes.\n\n- Wave interval increased from 25s to 30s (bounty values adjusted by the same ratio)\n- Guardians attack range reduced from 38.1m to 35m\n- Guardians melee attempt range reduced from 6.1m to 5m\n- Guardians melee damage range reduced from 8m to 7m\n- Players can no longer Parry troopers\n- Medic Trooper: Heal no longer has charges (was 3)\n- Medic Trooper: Cooldown reduced from 35s to 6s (this was the previous setup before)\n- Medic Trooper: Fixed healing sometimes not functioning on bounce when there are walls nearby\n- Troopers will no longer aggro to you when you shoot them while they are attacking a Guardian\n\n- Urn now reveals you immediately\n- First urn is now considered for comeback mechanics (15% NW delta)\n- Urn now has a visual indication on the model to indicate if its a side favored pickup\n- Urn will no longer wait indefinitely for someone nearby to pick him up. defeated\n- Fixed a bug where troopers would rapidly change targets when multiple enemies are in range\n- Fixed a bug where troopers would fidget with their facing direction when walking without a   "id": "2024-05-03",
````json
  "posted_at": "2024-05-03",
  "raw_content": "General Changes:\n==\n- Added a Recommend A Friend button to the dashboard that you can use to send us requests for people to include in our playtesting\n- Added a Resources page to the dashboard which contains a browsable item shop\n- Added overhead text display when another hero uses active items\n- The hotkeys F1-F5 to change cameras to allied heroes now maps directly to the order of heroes on the top bar left to right\n- Added the Patron to the spectate-when-dead cycle if the enemy is in your base or everyone on your team is dead\n- Added support for Flex Items in the Hero Sandbox\n\n- Increased the range of the mouse sensitivity slider from 0.5->4.0 to 0.05->8.0\n- Increased the default framerate cap from 120 to 400\n- Improved UI display when endgame objectives are being attacked\n- Added music for when the base is under attack\n- Changed the local player icon on the the minimap to always be on top of enemy icons\n- Shop music will now only play for shops players can access\n- Postgame graphs now default to team stats rather than individual\n- Added borders around hero icons on the minimap when watching replays/spectating\n- Top bar now shows incoming players before they are fully connected\n- Added console command to hide the bar at the bottom in replays ('citadel_hide_replay_hud 1' to hide it, 'citadel_hide_replay_hud 0' to bring it back)\n\n- Various VOIP improvements\n- Fixed open mic threshold\n- Fixed various consistency issues with the presentation of Souls terminology, icons, etc\n- Fixed Flying Cloak teleporting players into geometry or out of the world\n- Fixed Warp Stone teleporting you through geometry\n- Fixed Fixation decimal point display\n- Fixed Parry animation not playing\n- Fixed various issues with the spectate UI display\n- Removed the slow climbing vertical recoil on Yamato's alt fire\n- Fixed AP count not showing if you are dead, spectating a team mate or when holding alt/tab to upgrade your abilities\n\n\n\nGameplay Changes:\n==\n- Troopers DPS vs Lane Guardians reduced from 44 to 36\n- Objectives now have 80% damage reduction anti-backdoor defense when there haven't been creeps nearby in a while\n- The uncapturable zipline nodes near your base now extend out 2 more nodes (this means attackers can't capture ziplines quite as close to the enemy base)\n\n- You are now silenced while carrying the Urn\n- Urn bounty increased from 900 + 160/minute to 900 + 200/minute\n- Vaults base bounty increased from 150 to 200\n\n- Abrams: Base Health increased from 550 to 600\n- Abrams: Siphon DPS increased from 24 to 35\n\n- Bebop: Weapon no longer has horizontal/vertical recoil\n\n- Dynamo: Singularity DPS increased from 48 to 60\n- Dynamo: Singularity T3 Max HP DPS increased from 3.2% to 3.8%\n\n- Grey Talon: Charge Shot damage increased from 95 to 105\n- Grey Talon: Charge Shot T2 damage reduced from 80 to 70\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl damage increased from 200 to 300\n\n- Haze: Bullet Dance bonus Fire Rate reduced from +30 to +20\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb duration scaling from Spirit improved from 0.2 to 0.3\n\n- Infernus: Catalyst no longer slows Infernus to 1.3 m/s during the cast delay\n- Infernus: Concussive Combustion damage increased from 130 to 160\n\n- Kelvin: Frost Grenade T3 bonus damage increased from +100 to +175\n\n- Lady Geist: Blood Bomb damage increased from 80 to 100\n- Lady Geist: Blood Bomb T2 damage increased from +65 to +70\n- Lady Geist: Blood Bomb tooltip fixed to reference the correct self damage type and that it can be reduced with armor\n- Lady Geist: Malice damage amp per shard increased from 10% to 15%\n\n- Lash: Grapple cooldown reduced from 55 to 45\n- Lash: Flog Damage increased from 55 to 65\n- Lash: Flog lifesteal from heroes increased from 70% to 80% of damage dealt\n- Lash: Flog lifesteal from non-heroes reduced from 35% to 30% of damage dealt\n- Lash: Death Slam T1 from +3m to +5m\n- Lash: Fixed Death Slam's targeting cone not finding enemies near the edges\n- Lash: Fixed Death Slam to only target enemies who stay in the targeting cone\n\n- McGinnis: Heavy Barrage now reduces your speed rather than setting it to a low cap (by itself this change isn't a buff or a nerf, but it allows you to buy items to move faster during the ultimate)\n- McGinnis: Heavy Barrage camera interaction with Fleetfoot Boots has been fixed\n\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast range increased from 25m to 30m\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast width increased from 3m to 5m\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast is now permissive with small obstructions in the way\n- Mo & Krill: Burrow no longer loses its state as you change elevations\n- Mo & Krill: Burrow now knocks enemies up when you come up\n- Mo & Krill: Combo DPS increased from 50 to 60\n- Mo & Krill: No longer listed as a recommended new player hero\n\n- Vindicta: Base Bullet Damage reduced from 15 to 14\n- Vindicta: Bullet Damage gained per boon reduced from 0.88 to 0.7 (these are gained occasionally as you earn souls, up to 11 times)\n- Vindicta: Stake T1 bonus duration reduced from +1.5s to +1.0s\n- Vindicta: Is now a recommended hero for new players\n\n- Yamato: Crimson Slash radius increased from 11m to 12m\n- Yamato: Crimson Slash fire rate debuff duration increased from 3s to 4s\n- Yamato: Crimson Slash T2 changed from \"-5s Cooldown\" to \"10% Max Health heal on hero hit\"\n- Yamato: Crimson Slash T3 changed from \"12% Max Health heal on hero hit\" to \"-6s Cooldown\"\n- Yamato: Power Slash T1 Bullet Resist increased from 40% to 60%\n- Yamato: Flying Strike cooldown reduced from 35 to 20\n- Yamato: Flying Strike T2 changed from \"-15s Cooldown\" to \"+20 Cast Range\"\n- Yamato: Flying Strike range no longer scales with Spirit\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion bonus Spirit per victim increased from 10 to 15\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion bonus Fire Rate per victim increased from 5 to 10\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion buff duration increased from 8s to 15s",
  "title": "05-03-2024 Update",
````

- Source URL: `https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/05-10-2024-update.689/`
- Source Raw Path: `data/raw/deadlock_data/data_changelogs_raw_2024-05-10.txt.7a978ad2fd8d7499.txt`

### Vollstaendige Payload

````json
{
  "_deadlock_data": {
    "file_path": "data/changelogs/raw/2024-05-10.txt",
    "forum_id": "689",
    "is_hero_lab": false,
    "source_origin": "deadlock-wiki/deadlock-data",
    "source_trust": "trusted",
    "structured_events_path": "data/changelogs/versions/2024-05-10.json",
    "wiki_wikitext_path": "data/changelogs/wiki/2024-05-10.txt"
  },
  "id": "2024-05-10",
  "posted_at": "2024-05-10",
  "raw_content": "We’ve heard a lot of feedback on matchmaking quality recently, especially with parties that include a wide range of skill spread and the impact they have on matches. We think it’s important to try to improve these as best we can so everyone can have fun playing. Options are a bit limited right now in general due to small queue sizes, but we have a few adjustments we are doing in today’s update that we hope will give us more potential to optimize and tweak the system in the immediate future. These changes will need continual tuning over the coming days, so the impact may not be fully visible today.\n\n- The match will no longer start at a fixed time immediately after 12 players are in the queue, sometimes it might start faster sometimes it might start slower, depending on the distribution of players in the queue. There will still be an upper bound for how long it waits, for practical reasons, but it won’t automatically start asap if it knows the match won’t be good. Conversely, if the match is already reasonable it won’t wait unnecessarily to start it.​\n​\n- Parties that have too high of a gap between the players in them will often make for a bad experience for both teams, but especially the opponents. When we detect that your party has this composition, we will present a warning letting you know that this will result in a more difficult match for your party and might also take longer to find a game, so you can decide if you value the group/social aspect more or not. In terms of specific matchmaking changes, in cases where the party has a large gap in skill, the party’s overall skill will now be biased closer to the best player’s values rather than the average. This will make it less likely that the system grabs lower skilled players for the opposing team just because your party was ok with wide variance.​\n\n\nThis update also has a couple of fixes that we are moving up from our usual end of week patch:\n\n- We fixed a bug when combining shields and resistance that caused you to take too much damage. Previously there was a bug where if you take a high amount of damage, the remaining damage after shield reduction would pass through as pure damage instead of being reduced by resist. An example of this is someone dealing 800 spirit damage to you and you having 80 Spirit Shield and 45% Spirit Resistance. With the bug, the 800 would be reduced to 720 from the shield, and then you would lose 720 health. With the fix, the shield now reduces the damage to 720 as usual, but the remaining amount then gets reduced by 45% to 396 health lost instead of 720.​\n​\n- We are also nerfing Alchemical Fire. It now deals 40% less damage to non-heroes and the cooldown is increased from 25s to 37s.​",
  "title": "05-10-2024 Update",
  "url": "https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/05-10-2024-update.689/"
}
````

````json
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Sandbox mode now supports entering it with your party\n- Sandbox mode now has troopers spawning on the left side\n\n- Added Search By Match ID to the Watch Tab\n- Added Filter By Hero to the Watch Tab\n\n- You can now see an indicator for enemy troopers when near them if they are behind a wall or offscreen (primarly improves tracking their positions during the lane)\n\n- Map is now precached while in the queue to improve loading times into matches\n- Visual and Audio updates to the shop\n- Added a key for toggling replays being paused (defaults to P)\n- Pocket Affliction visual and sound effects reworked\n- Pocket Enchanter’s Satchel visual effects updated\n- Fixed a bug where Pocket’s briefcase would stop animating when the duration upgrade was taken\n- Updated Divine Barrier sound effects\n- Updated Dynamo VO for Quantum Entanglement and a few other missing lines\n- Hero death voices are now broadcast more widely\n- Fixed being unable to ping while casting or channeling\n- Fixed various issues with hero abilities causing you to be stuck in walls\n- Fixed players being able to kill themselves pre-match and run around in the map\n- Fixed Ice Dome effects disappearing while still active if Kelvin dies during it\n- Simplify some visuals with Seven’s Storm Cloud radius indicator to help clarify the bounds\n- Fixed Stamina not appearing to regenerate while spectating\n- Improve the text display when a replay is still processing after a match\n- Fixed Walker boss moving around while the game is paused\n- Fixed some controller input issues\n- Fortitude now starts on cooldown to prevent purchasing and selling in the field for significant regen\n- Updated Decay item sound effects\n- Updated Berserker item visuals\n- Fixed visual bug on Spirit Urn when dropping on the ground\n- Fixed Restorative Locket Spirit Resist not working\n- Fixed items not updating in the UI when purchased from the shop while spectating a teammate\n\n\n\n[ Item Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Spirit Strike: Buff duration increased from 12s to 18s\n- Spirit Strike: Melee damage bonus increased from 15% to 20%\n- Hollow Point Ward: Spirit Shield increased from +70 to +80\n- Melee Lifesteal: Cooldown reduced from 10s to 8s\n- Alchemical Fire: Min/Max DPS increased from 30/60 to 40/80\n- Restorative Locket: Max Stacks increased from 12 to 15\n- Restorative Locket: Cooldown increased from 15s to 18s\n- Reactive Barrier: Barrier duration increased from 10s to 13s\n- Reactive Barrier: Cooldown reduced from 60s to 40s\n- Return Fire: No longer has Spirit and Fire Rate bonus during the active\n- Return Fire: Now passively always gives +7 Spirit and +7% Fire Rate\n- Slowing Hex: Cooldown reduced from 30s to 25s\n- Decay: Cooldown reduced from 25s to 22s\n- Decay: Cast range increased from 20m to 30m\n- Enduring Speed: Movement Speed increased from 1 to 1.25\n- Suppressor: Now provides +50 Health\n- Quicksilver: Now passively has a +10% reload time penalty\n- Divine Barrier: No longer restores any Stamina on the target (previously fully restored it)\n- Warp Stone: No longer slows your hero for 3s\n- Warp Stone: Bullet Resist duration increased from 3s to 5s\n- Withering Whip: Cast range increased from 12m to 20m\n- Knockdown: Cooldown reduced from 55s to 50s\n- Knockdown: Now provides +200 Spirit Shield\n- Escalating Resilience: Resist per stack increased from 1% to 2%\n- Superior Stamina: Spirit buff duration increased from 5s to 8s\n- Lifestrike: Slow duration increased from 1s to 1.5s\n- Lifestrike: Cooldown reduced from 10s to 8s\n- Heroic Aura: Active radius increased from 20m to 30m\n- Curse: Now provides +15% Clip\n- Curse: Duration increased from 3.5s to 3.75s\n- Shadow Weave: Now provides +15 Health Regen\n- Phantom Strike: No longer passively provides +500 Bullet Shield\n- Phantom Strike: Now provides +200 Health\n- Phantom Strike: When using the active, gain a temporary +500 Bullet Shield for 7 seconds\n- Phantom Strike: Now deals 200 damage on impact\n- Vampiric Burst: Active Bullet Lifesteal increased from 40% to 60%\n- Unstoppable: Movement Speed increased from +1 to +1.25\n- Mystic Reverb: Spirit Lifesteal increased from +10% to +15%\n- Mystic Reverb: Spirit Resist increased from +10% to +15%\n- Crippling Headshot: Headshot Resistance Reduction increased from 30% to 35%\n- Diviner's Kevlar: Channeling Spirit bonus increased from +40 to +50\n\n\n\n[ Hero Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Abrams: Reduced chances that Leap gets stuck on geometry during the downward phase\n\n- Bebop: No longer has an innate +15% Spirit Resist\n- Bebop: Now has innate +20% Bullet Resist\n- Bebop: Hook cooldown reduced from 27s to 22s\n- Bebop: Hook T3 reduced from -15s to -12s Cooldown\n- Bebop: Hook Speed increased from 2700 to 3000\n- Bebop: Hook Return Speed increased from 1800 to 2200\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam DPS increased from 180 to 210\n\n- Grey Talon: Charged Shot damage increased from 105 to 115\n- Grey Talon: Rain of Arrows Multishot increased from 3 to 5\n\n- Kelvin: Frost Grenade cooldown reduced from 30s to 26s\n\n- Infernus: Fixed Afterburn refreshing from item damage\n\n- Ivy: Base stamina increased from 3 to 4\n\n- Lash: Ground Strike now additionally damages enemies within 2m of Lash on impact\n- Lash: Ground Strike radius reduced from 16m to 14m\n- Lash: Ground Strike's ground preview now lines up under Lash when cast on the ground\n- Lash: Ground Strike T3 reduced from +100% to +85%\n- Lash: Bullet Damage growth increased from 0.76 to 1.0\n- Lash: Death Slam tether visuals now improved to communicate the different phases its in\n\n- Lady Geist: Malice now comes out quicker and no longer slows you\n\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret Spirit Resistance increased 50% to 60%\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret Health increased from 30% to 40%\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret T2 increased from +5m Range to +10m\n- McGinnis: Fixed issues with Heavy Barrage not damaging units within the target area\n\n- Mo & Krill: Base HP increased from 650 to 700\n- Mo & Krill: Scorn damage increased from 40 to 50\n- Mo & Krill: Scorn T3 debuff duration increased from 10s to 16s\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast duration increased from 3.5s to 4s\n- Mo & Krill: Combo T3 DPS increased from +30 to +40\n\n- Paradox: Time Wall bullet freeze duration no longer increases with Spirit or Duration Extender\n- Paradox: Time Wall bullet freeze base duration reduced from 1.5 to 1.25\n- Paradox: Time Wall no longer deals damage to Mid Boss\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine: Max Damage Amp reduced from 150% to 125%\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine T3 no longer provides +50% Min Damage Amp\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine T3 reduced from +125% Max Damage to +75%\n\n- Pocket: Gun damage per bullet reduced from 6 to 5.6\n- Pocket: Affliction DPS reduced from 30 to 26\n\n- Seven: Power Surge rebalanced around no longer reducing fire rate (similar shock value effectiveness as before)\n\n- Warden: Alchemical Flask damage increased from 48 to 55\n\n- Yamato: Crimson Slash cooldown reduced from 13s to 11s\n- Yamato: Power Slash physics/world collision reduced from 15 to 4 (allowing it to be more easily used near objects)\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion radius increased from 14m to 16m\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion Fire Rate and Spirit buff duration increased from 15s to 20s\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion T3 Spirit bonus increased from +10% to +15%\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion T3 Fire Rate bonus increased from +5% to +10%\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Sprint is now always considered active as long as you are out of combat (haven’t taken or dealt damage for 5s, healthbar is now a little transparent when out of combat).\n- Fixed various inconsistencies and scenarios with how Sprint speed was calculated. Previously there were many conditions that could result in you losing the bonus.\n- Patron (Weakened) now has 40hp/s regen when it has not taken damage for 30s\n- Medic Troopers no longer use their heal on troopers if there is a hero within 30m\n- Zipline Speed Boost cooldown reduced from 420s to 380s\n- Damage over time no longer prevents zipline usage\n- Adjusted how the Anti Backdoor system works. It does a little less % damage reduction now, but it regens “backdoor damage” dealt to it over time. Backdoor damage is defined as any damage taken with no candle troopers within 50m in the last 20 seconds.\n- Troopers run speed increased from 9.1 m/s to 10 m/s\n- Troopers DPS vs Patrons reduced from 110 DPS to 80 DPS\n- Troopers DPS vs Walkers from 110 DPS to 80 DPS\n- Troopers DPS vs Base Guardians from 110 DPS to 80 DPS\n- Crate Powerups: Removed Zipline buff as a potential drop\n- Crate Powerups: Increased Health Regen drop from 1.25% Regen to 1.5%\n- Crate Powerups: Added Fire Rate drop, provides +20% Fire Rate for 25s\n- Vault base bounty increased from 200 to 225\n- Fixed some issues that allowed players to more easily camp players in the spawn area\n\n\n\nNote: 10% of users will default to vulkan rendersystem. If you experience any issues related to this, you can report these on the forums and switch to dx11 in the video settings. Deadlock will eventually drop support for dx11 and render on vulkan exclusively.",
````

````json
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Playtesting times are now starting 1 hour earlier. Weekdays are 6PM PDT and weekends are 4PM PDT.\n\n- Redesigned the Walker model and visuals\n\n- Added ambient sound effects\n- Added jar smash sound effects\n- Updated Cold Front sound effects\n- Improved NPC/minion weapon fire audio feedback for victims\n- Improved player damage received audio feedback\n- Updated Lash Death Slam sound effects\n- Various minor fixes to Ground Strike and Death Slam effects\n- Warden's Last Stand has improved visual effects\n- Fixed a bug where the shopping book visuals could get stuck on your hero\n- Vindicta's Crow Familiar projectile effect indicates the radius more accurately now\n- New voice for Warden (in advance of upcoming model swap)\n- Patrons will now play a voice warning when Rejuvenator time is close to expiring as well as when it's expired\n- Updated VO for Amber Hand's Patron\n- Increased the duration of the item-used popup text a bit\n- Fixed a bug where Warden's second and third abilities were swapped VO wise\n- Fixed Time Wall damaging units that don't move multiple times instead of once per walk through\n- Added a short wall between Shrines level of the base and the Weakened Patron level\n- Moved the taxi back into the street to fix players getting stuck between the front and the building corner\n- Moved a truck away from the garage to give wide enough path for Mo & Krill\n- Added clips to both sides of the overpass on outer lanes to keep from hitting your head on the ledge when climbing\n- Added clips at outer lane T1 stairs where you could get stuck doing a roll slide\n- Fixed Vindicta being able to shoot above the base wall\n- Fixed gaps where you could climb outside of the map\n- Added a wall between the T1 shop and the hallway on the outer lanes by the Fire House and the Chapel to prevent attackers from shooting the T1 without taking damage\n- Fixed a couple of spots where Mo could get stuck between a truck and wall\n- Replaced news stand that was close to the T1 with different cover kiosk building to avoid shop confusion\n- Fixed no collision on water tower on the garage roof.\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Medium and Hard neutrals now spawn at 7 minutes\n- Neutrals now have 20% Spirit Resist\n- Basement neutrals now have +2 Medium neutrals and -4 Small neutrals\n- Basement neutrals are now classified as a Medium camp instead of a Small camp (for the purposes of spawn time)\n- Fixed various abuses where you can shoot neutrals without them being able to shoot back. Neutrals now become invulnerable when they have no line of sight of you for 2 seconds.\n\n- Spirit scaling reduced by ~19% (the total effective Spirit damage is reduced by less than this, this is just the Spirit multiplier scaling portion and has largest impact on late game burst)\n- Respawn curve adjusted slightly (results in a few seconds of increased respawn time in the mid phase, same overall max respawn time)\n- Zipline Boost ability is now available at the start of the game (used to start on cooldown)\n- Shield break cooldown reduced from 80s to 60s\n- Troopers run speed increased from 10 m/s to 11 m/s\n- Medic Trooper health increased from 200 to 240\n\n\n\n[ Item Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Added a new T3 Spirit Item, Shifting Shroud. Innate: +10% Fire Rate. Active: You become invulnerable for 2.5s, during which you cannot move or perform any actions. When your invulnerability expires, your gun is instantly reloaded and you gain 14 bonus Spirit for 6s. Cooldown: 70s.\n\n- Mystic Burst: Bonus damage reduced from +40 to +35\n- Medic Bullets: Cooldown reduced from 5s to 4s\n- Headshot Booster: Headshot damage increased from +30 to +50\n- Close Quarters: Bullet Shield increased from 70 to 85\n- Close Quarters: Weapon Damage reduced from 30% to 25%\n- Healing Rite: Cooldown reduced from 90s to 80s\n\n- Kinetic Dash: No longer gives +1 Stamina\n- Kinetic Dash: Now causes your dash-jumps to only require 1 stamina\n- Berserker: Weapon Damage Per Stack increased from 5% to 6%\n- Mystic Shot: Cooldown increased from 3s to 5s\n- Reactive Barrier: Now provides a Spirit Shield instead of a Bullet Shield\n- Health Nova: Now provides +50 Health\n- Return Fire: Now also reflects 30% of Spirit damage (requires line of sight)\n- Return Fire: Bullet damage returned reduced from 75% to 60%\n- Return Fire: Fixed not returning fire to McGinnis' sentries\n- Silence Glyph: Now applies 8% Spirit Amp instead of -15% Bullet Resist\n- Silence Glyph: Cooldown increased from 20s to 35s\n- Decay: Now does 2.2% Max HP DPS instead of flat DPS\n- Restorative Locket: Now gives +4 Spirit\n\n- Alchemical Fire: Moved from T2 to T3\n- Alchemical Fire: Now grants +150 Bullet Shield\n- Alchemical Fire: Now grants +12% Weapon Damage\n- Alchemical Fire: Min/Max DPS increased from 40/80 to 45/85\n- Majestic Leap: You can now use the active again while in the air to dive down faster\n- Improved Burst: Max HP bonus damage reduced from 13% to 11%\n- Knockdown: Cast range increased from 40m to 50m\n- Mystic Slow: Now requires Suppressor as a component\n- Mystic Slow: Now provides +75 Health, +3 Regen and +6 spirit\n- Mystic Slow: Fire Rate slow increased from 20% to 35%\n- Lifestrike: Cooldown reduced from 8s to 5s\n- Withering Whip: Now applies -25% Bullet Resist instead of 25% Spirit Amp\n- Withering Whip: Now grants +15% Ammo\n- Warp Stone: Removed cast time\n\n- Diviner's Kevlar: Now triggers off of casting ultimates. Grants 700 bullet shield and 40 spirit for 10 seconds.\n- Echo Shard: No longer gives +20 Spirit for the second cast\n- Mystic Reverb: Extra damage on the reverb reduced from 40% to 35%\n- Frenzy: Bonus health increased from +150 to +200\n\n\n\n[ Hero Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Bebop: Sticky Bomb T3 now applies the silence on cast instead of detonation\n- Bebop: Sticky Bomb T3 silence duration reduced from 5s to 3.5s\n- Bebop: Sticky Bomb now uses a new instant cast mechanism. When you are in range there is a valid target indicator.\n- Kelvin: Now has +10% Base Spirit Resistance\n- Kelvin: Frozen Shelter T1 increased from +35% to +40% Fire Rate Slow\n- Kelvin: Ice Path T3 Now gains 1 Spirit per meter of Ice Path trail created. Max of 40 Spirit.\n- Grey Talon: Charged Shot cooldown reduced from 18s to 16s\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger cooldown increased from 25s to 27s\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb cooldown reduced from 45s to 35s\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb T1 changed to +4 Invis Sprint\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb T2 is now -20s Cooldown\n- Infernus: Afterburn T2 now reduces enemy Spirit damage by 30%\n- Ivy: True Form cooldown reduced from 50s to 40s\n- Lady Geist: Soul Exchange now uses a new instant cast mechanism. When you are in range there is a valid target indicator.\n- Lash: Ground Strike Stomp Damage reduced from 125 to 110\n- Lash: Death Slam cooldown increased from 110s to 130s\n- Lash: Grapple Bullet Damage bonus increased from +4 to +6\n- Lash: Grapple cooldown decreased from 45s to 40s\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret T1 is now +1 Charge and +25% Movement Slow\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret T2 is now +10m Attack Range and +10% Fire Rate\n- McGinnis: Spectral Wall cooldown reduced from 40s to 35s\n- Mo & Krill: Combo now uses a new instant cast mechanism. When you are in range there is a valid target indicator.\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine T3 reduced from +75% to +50%\n- Vindicta: Flight spirit bullet damage now has range effectiveness (previously the magic bullets did max damage from any range)\n- Vindicta: Flight spirit damage per bullet increased from 8 to 12\n- Vindicta: Flight T2 bonus spirit damage per bullet increased from 8 to 12\n- Warden: Last Stand no longer goes on cooldown when it is interrupted during its initial cast delay\n- Yamato: Shadow Explosion cooldown reduced from 120s to 90s",
````

````json
  "raw_content": "=== General ===\n\n\n* Base HP reduced by 10 for all heroes\n\n* HP per boon reduced by 3\n\n\n* Guardians scaling resistance from +75%->-50% over 12 minutes to +50%->-50% (takes more damage early)\n\n* Medium neutrals initial spawn time reduced from 6 minutes to 5 minutes\n\n* Breakables spawn time increased from 2 minutes to 3 minutes\n\n\n* Shrines are now invulnerable until one pair of Base Guardians are destroyed\n\n* Backdoor removal linger duration reduced from 20s to 14s\n\n\n* Knockup durations are now affected by debuff reduction\n\n\n* Removed two jump pads from around the middle of the map that point to outwards (to the left and right respectively)\n\n* Sliding no longer resets sprint speed\n\n\n* Stamina Regeneration is reduced by 25% for 5s after using a Wall Jump. Resistance increased from 10% to 20%\n\n* Shrine Bullet Resistance increased from 10% to 20%\n\n* Tier 2 Bullet Resistance increased from 25% to 35%\n\n\n* Midboss now starts spawned at the beginning of the game\n\n\n* Total Damage Reduction debuffs now stack diminishingly rather than additively (Inhibitor, Fire Scarabs, etc)\n\n\n* Movespeed diminish point increased from 11 to 12 (very slightly less aggressive diminishing scaling)\n\n\n* Weapon Investment Bonus now has a 6.4k line, changed from 4.8k/7.2k/9.6k/16k to 4.8k/6.4k/8k/11.2k/16k with bonuses from 46/55/70/85 to 46/54/62/74/86\n\n* Vitality Investment Bonus now has a 6.4k line, changed from 4.8k/7.2k/9.6k/16k to 4.8k/6.4k/8k/11.2k/16k with bonuses from 38/42/46/50 to 38/42/46/50 (final two tiers shifted up by +4)\n\n* Spirit Investment Bonus now has a 6.4k line, changed from 4.8k/7.2k/9.6k/16k to 4.8k/6.4k/8k/11.2k/16k with bonuses from 38/48/57/66 to 38/45/52/59/66\n\n\n* Fixed various item barrier durations not scaling with duration increases\n\n* Walkers will interrupt a rocket barrage during the 1s windup if stomp becomes an option.\n\n* Fixed a bug where walkers were spinning while lasering.\n\n* Smoothed zipline grabbing to fix some jitter\n\n* Added ropes to zipline binding (so if a player rebinds zipline to something other than SPACE it will also apply to mounting/dismounting ropes)\n\n* Removed delay on ziplines when using alternative key binds\n\n\n* Fixed the following abilities taking you out of zoom temporarily when cast: Blasted, Gloom Bombs, Shining Wonder, Call Bell, Luggage Cart, Stalker's Mark, Rejuvenating Aurora, Charged Shot, Powder Keg, Bounce Pad, Spirit Lasso, Napalm, Ice Path, Flog, Spectral Wall, Rake, Fire Scarabs, Scorn, Sand Blast, Time Wall, Paradoxical Swap, Barrage, Flying Cloak, Pillow Toss, Lightning Ball, Entangling Bola, Go For The Throat, Vexing Bolt, Consecrating Grenade, Gut Shot, Snap Trap, Ira Domini, Pain Battery, Jumpstart, Flight, Crow Familiar, Lethal Venom, Petrifying Bola, Alchemical Flask, Card Trick, Capacitor, Restorative Locket, Healing Nova, Silence Wave, Magic Carpet, Refresher\n\n\n* Fixed the following items and abilities not working through veils: Heroic Aura, Healing Rite, Healing Nova, Rescue Beam, Divine Barrier, Kudzu Connection, Cube, Tag Along, Lil Helpers, Guardian Ward, Scourge, Plot Armor\n\n\n\n=== Soul Urn ===\n\n\n* We are experimenting with an alternate set of Urn mechanics. Please give us your thoughts on this after you've played with it some.\n\n\nPrimary Rules:\n\n* To pickup the Urn, rather than standing in place to channel, you now light or heavy melee the Urn to pick it up.\n\n* The Urn is now always dropped off on top of the bridge on the middle of the map.\n\n* Once dropped off, the Urn will go into a \"Depositing\" phase for a set amount of time, depending on which team the urn favors (3s/5s/10s for Favored/Neutral/Unfavored).\n\n* While in the Depositing phase, the enemy team can Heavy Melee the urn to have their team claim it and cause it to switch sides. This adds +1.25s to that team's timer.\n\n* If the urn is Favored or Unfavored (in a comeback state), then the Favored team will get +50% Bullet and Spirit Resist in a 60m radius around the urn while it is being carried, dropped or deposited.\n\n\n\nOther Details:\n\n* After 35s (aggregate time held per team), the carrier will start taking 5% Max HP damage per second (previously was 45s and 0.15% Max HP damage).\n\n* Urn damage is lethal\n\n* Like before, when the Urn is fumbled, it will wait 13s if there is a player within 25m. However afterwards, with this new version, it can no longer be picked up anymore and will very quickly run back to its spawn position.\n\n* No longer silences you while carrying. You are still disarmed and movement silenced.\n\n* No longer grants +30% Bullet and Spirit Resist while carrying it\n\n* Area around deposting the urn is revealed (urn runner is also revealed as usual)\n\n* The rules for Favored/Unfavored remain the same (+15% soul difference for the first urn at 10:00, +10% for all future urns)\n\n* Souls are all instant after the deposit is complete\n\n* All rewards are the same. The last person to contest the urn gets the +3 Golden Idol buffs, falling back to the original carrier if nobody contested it.\n\n\n\n\n\n=== Items ===\n\n\n* Added a new T1 Vitality Item, Grit. Active grants you 200 Barrier for 4s duration. Innate grants 1 Out of Combat Regen. Cooldown: 60s.   "posted_at": "2024-05-30",
````
````json
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Bots will now replace a human player if the player has been disconnected for more than 30 seconds (hero will retreat home first and then after 30 seconds the bot takes over). After 4 minutes it will start using souls to buy items. During laning phase it will go to its assigned lane, afterwards it will aim to stick near where most allies are located. If the player rejoins the bot will leave. We will work on improving the bot behavior as we get feedback.\n- Added a temporary matchmaking ban system for people that leave games\n\n- Reworked the Watch tab to highlight suggested matches to watch. There is currently a limit on the number of people that can watch the same match, so it'll recommend different games if the best suggestions are full. We are working on removing the spectator limit for a future update.\n- Matches in the watch tab will now indicate if a friend is in that match\n\n- A paused a game can only be unpaused by the player that paused for the first 30 seconds\n- Once you pause a game, you cannot pause it again within 5 minutes\n- Pause now says the player name pausing it\n\n- Added a view cone for your player on the minimap to help with finding yourself and the direction you are pointed\n- Added a sound when you receive a chat message\n- Increased the base font size for text chat\n- Increased the duration text chat is visible\n- You can now ping enemy items on the scoreboard to say alert players that the hero has that item.\n- You can now middle mouse click an ability to say its on cooldown\n- You can now alt click your active items to say if they are ready or not\n- Candle Troopers and other NPCS can no longer see or shoot through Cosmic Veils\n- Changed F1-F5 keys to instantly snap to the team mates rather than move there more slowly\n- Adjusted the health bar on Walkers to be easier to see\n- Match found sound now plays regardless of window focus\n- Added option to give fast stamina regen in Hero Sandbox\n- Minimap player icons now dynamically shrink and adjust positions slightly when there are many overlapping\n- Fixed zooming bypassing the shooting speed penalty\n- Added new effects for Shifting Shroud\n- T1 Boss now has quicker melee animations\n- Fixed Turrets causing backdoor protection to be lost\n\n- Fixed Guided Owl flying while the game is paused\n- Fixed a bug with using Melee in the air that could cause you to move a lot\n- Fixed some sound issues with Pulse Grenade\n- Added new sprint animations for Abrams\n- Various fixes to Lash's Ground Strike visual effects\n- Fixed various bugs and crashes with hero guides\n- Fixed Turrets not proccing things like Mystic Vulnerability\n- Fixed some outdated details on the minimap\n- Grey Talon's Charged Shot effect matches the damage radius better\n- Added visual tell for when Assassinate is active\n- Fixed some bugs that could cause McGinnis gun to be stuck\n- Updated Zipline Boost hint to say ACTIVE when it is active and only show the cooldown timer when its done\n- Fixed an edge case with Unstoppable and a debuff being applied on the same frame\n- Reduced minimum delay before you can press a button again to cancel a channel/precast from 0.5 to 0.2\n- Fixed getting stuck going up the ramp on the inner lane street\n- Fixed corner neutral camps not burrowing if they lose line of sight\n- Fixed being able to get on top of the tall border building behind the Chapel and behind the Firehouse\n\n\n\n[Misc Gameplay]\n\n- Lane Guardian HP increased from 3750 to 4600\n- Various adjustments to lane layout to give defenders more options for forward positions near their Guardian\n- Midlane Walkers HP reduced a little bit to match the same HP as the sidelane Walkers\n- Base Guardians moved a little bit further towards the outside of the base\n- Airborne Heavy Melee now triggers when the charge up is complete, rather than waiting for the player to land on the ground (you had to manually release it before)\n- Airborne Heavy Melee distance increased to match ground version\n- Heavy Melee time reduced from 0.6 to 0.55 (same for Air)\n- Crate powerups can now only spawn after 2 minutes (when the small neutrals spawn)\n- Added breakable crates inside each lane's Cosmic Veil\n- Expanded space in some of the lane's Cosmic Veils\n- Audible distance of enemy footsteps is reduced\n- Special 15 minute melee creep now upgrades again at 25 minutes\n- Base zipline speed increased by 10%\n\n- Extended inner lane urn bridge buildings toward the T1 and added a doorway to allow defenders to shoot down into the canal\n- Removed 3 bush trees on each inner lane near the urn bridge buildings\n- Carved out interior of inner lane tower buildings beside the Guardian and connected to the neutral camp in the underground areas\n- Moved outer lane Walker on Factory and Mansion sides slightly closer to the stairs\n- Carved out interior of outer lane wall buildings beside the Walker to allow forward position for defenders\n- Reduced width of forward base lookout bridges\n\n\n\n[Item Gameplay]\n\n- Basic Magazine: Ammo reduced from +16% to +15%\n- Basic Magazine: Weapon Damage reduced from +16% to +15%\n- Headshot Booster: Cooldown reduced from 9s to 5s\n- Spirit Strike: Health Regen increased from 0.7 to 1\n- Enduring Spirit: No longer provides +1 Sprint\n- Enduring Spirit: +10% Spirit Lifesteal\n- Soul Shredder Bullets: Spirit Amp reduced from 14% to 12%\n- Slowing Hex: Cooldown increased from 25 to 27\n- Restorative Locket: Spirit increased from +4 to +5\n- Decay: Cast range now scales with Spirit (0.2 factor)\n- Suppressor: Fire rate slow increased from 30% to 35%\n- Enduring Speed: Move Speed increased from +1.25 to +1.4\n- Swift Striker: Bullet Resist penalty reduced from -8% to -7%\n- Alchemical Fire: Now grants +5 Spirit\n- Alchemical Fire: Bullet Shield Health increased from +150 to +175\n- Alchemical Fire: Cooldown reduced from 37 to 34\n- Shifting Shroud: Duration increased from 2.75 to 3\n- Shifting Shroud: Can now be canceled early\n- Shifting Shroud: Cooldown reduced from 55s to 45s\n- Knockdown: Cooldown reduced from 50s to 40s\n- Superior Stamina: Spirit buff duration increased from 8s to 12s\n- Lifestrike: Melee damage increased from +35% to +40%\n- Toxic Bullets: Now deals half damage against troopers and neutrals\n- Leech: No longer gives +8 Lifesteal increased from 60% to 70%\n- Shadow Weave: Invis Sprint increased from +3 to +5\n- Curse: Now grants +8 Spirit\n- Refresher: Spirit Resist increased from +10% to +12%\n- Silencer: Ammo increased from +15% to +20%\n- Spiritual Overflow: Spirit bonus increased from 30 to 35\n- Boundless Spirit: Spirit increased from +40 to +45\n\n\n\n[Hero Gameplay]\n\n- Abrams: Seismic Impact is now Talon: Guided Owl duration increased from 13s to 14s\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl T3 increased from 20% to 22%\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl player collision sized increased to regular sizes for these kind of projectiles\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger cooldown increased from 27 to 29\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger spirit scaling increased from 2.2 to 2.5\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb T2 and T3 swapped\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb T3 from +30% to +35%\n- Infernus: Flame Dash T2 increased from +40 DPS to +45\n- Infernus: Flame Dash T3 increased from -15s to -20s\n- Ivy: Kudzu Bomb T3 improved from +50 DPS to +60\n- Kelvin: Base Path\n- Kelvin: Ice Path slow resistance increased from 50% to 60%\n- Kelvin: Ice Path T3 max spirit increased from 40 to 55\n- Lady Geist: Soul Exchange cast time reduced from 0.5 to 0.2\n- McGinnis: Rocket Barrage now does less damage to objectives similar to other ability damage\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast no longer affects bosses\n- Paradox: Bullet base damage reduced from 8 to 7.2 (indirectly Swap range reduced from 40m to 35m\n- Paradox: Fixed Paradoxical Swap being able to grab multiple heroes if they were really close to each other\n- Pocket: Barrage radius increased from 5m to 6m\n- Pocket: Barrage cooldown reduced from 35 to 30\n- Pocket: Barrage T3 increased from 100% to 150%\n- Pocket: Flying Cloak speed increased by 7%\n- Pocket: Flying Cloak T3 increased from +5 damage to +7\n- Vindicta: Now has -10% Base Bullet Resist\n- Vindicta: Flight no longer grants +1 bonus air speed\n- Vindicta: Flight falloff damage increase reduced from +20% to +10%\n- Vindicta: Fixed flight spirit damage being able to pierce Metal Skin\n- Warden: Willpower T1 improved from +15% to +20%\n- Warden: Last Stand duration increased from 5s to 6s\n- Wraith: Project Mind cooldown reduced from 50s to 45s\n- Yamato: Power Slash spirit scaling improved from 1.6 to 2.0\n- Yamato: Power Slash no longer does a very minor movement on enemies hit\n- Yamato: Fixed Power Slash sometimes not being a full charge when channeled fully during the Shadow Transformation\n- Yamato: Shadow Transformation's ability speedup now affects the post-cast durations as well\n- Yamato: Shadow Transformation's ability speedup now improves the visual animations (cosmetic only change)\n",
  "title": "05-30-2024 Update",
````

````json
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Reworked how friend invites work inside the client. You must now be Steam friends with a person to invite them. This has a friend recency requirement as well as your friend needing to not be a limited steam user. Previous requests do not need to be resubmitted.\n\n- Replaced the neutral vaults with a new model, the Sinner's Sacrifice\n- Added the ability to change builds while in-game\n- Build descriptions can now be seen in-game via a tooltip\n- Added neutrals to Hero Sandbox (the small room in the back on the right)\n- Added new objective health bars for Guardians and Walkers\n\n- Added Match History link to the top right\n- You can now move active item slots on the HUD while holding alt/tab\n- Improved visibility of the voice icon indicating who is talking\n- Hero icons on the minimap are scaled up a little when holding alt\n- Removed Pause cooldown in private bot matches and tutorial/sandbox\n\n- Improved networking when there is packet loss or jitter\n- Soul Vessel dropped from heroes (after killing neutrals) now scales in size based on how many souls are inside\n- Improved Grey Talon's trap visibility\n- Increased the speed of Guardian's melee attacks\n- Changed Guardian's behavior to only attempt melee when a unit is within 6m but still do damage to units in 12m range\n- Candle trooper health bars now stick around at 1% when they are in self destruct mode to make it more clear they can still be shot\n- Infernus's Afterburn now shows portraits of enemies that are burning for the full duration\n- Improved menu UI sound feedback\n- Player's icon is highlighted in the top bar\n- Added UI sound effects for team and opponent deaths\n- Added Kinetic Carbine charge audio loop\n- Updated Vindicta Flight sound effects\n- Added Vindicta Assassinate sound effect for targeted players\n- Improved visuals for Kelvin's gun projectile\n- Fixed some visual issues with Ice Path\n- Added AoE effect to Pocket's Barrage\n- Updated weapon buff effect in Pocket's Flying Cloak upgrade\n- Visual effects updates for melee lifesteal items\n- Fixed Power Slash sometimes getting stuck on nearby geometry\n- Updated sound effects for the Sinner's Sacrifice\n- Updated Pocket's Enchanted Satchel delay and explode sounds\n- Updated Pocket's Barrage cast and impact sounds\n- Updated Warden's Binding Word delay and impact sounds\n\n- Fixed Dynamo's Quantum Entanglement going through ceilings sometimes\n- Fixed an HTML exploit with chat\n- Fixed not being able to place deployable abilities at certain angles\n- Fixed moving active item slots causing the player to say \"[ITEM] is on cooldown\"\n- Fixed training Ivy's True Form causing you to get healed\n- Fixed a bug with Malice firing slightly upward\n- Changed collision on barrel stand stacks to block bullets\n- Added zapper on factory outpost building\n- Removed roof duct on top of low building besides inner Walker\n- Removed street lamp post that was causing heroes to get stuck near inner lane Guardian\n- Added lights to outer lane Cosmic Veil tunnels\n- More heroes will now compliment on good uses of ultimates instead of just saying \"good work\"\n- Fixed more instances of heroes being called out by the wrong name\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Base hero kill value increased from 150 to 225\n\n- Rejuv duration reduced from 4 to 3 minutes\n- Rejuv buff no longer increases your ammo\n- Rejuv now causes all your candle troopers to have double health for 3 minutes\n- Mid Boss can now only be damaged by enemies inside the bottom pit\n- Mid Boss Rejuv claim time reduced from 2.5s to 1.5s\n\n- Strong neutrals HP reduced by 20%\n- Strong neutrals souls bounty reduced by 20%\n- 4 Strong Neutral camps in the corners of the map have been replaced with Medium neutrals\n- Vaults souls increased from 260 to 300\n\n- Spirit tier bonus increased from 3/6/9/12 to 4/8/12/16\n- Vitality tier bonus increased from 5/10/15/20% to 8/12/16/20%\n- Bullet damage falloff now maxes out at ~65m instead of ~70m\n- Walker bomb damage reduced from 300 to 200\n- Moved the middle lane Base Guardians further out slightly\n\n- Powerups bonus types are now split between the regular destructibles like crates/vases/etc and the Golden Statues. Regular ones can only roll for souls and Golden Statues can only roll for buffs. Distribution of destructibles and Golden Status around the map has been adjusted a bit.\n- Respawn rate for Destructibles/Statues (after initial spawn) slowed from 120s to 180s\n- Mid boss area has reduced destructibles\n- Movement Speed powerup duration increased from 12s to 30s\n- Movement Speed powerup movespeed reduced from 2 to 1\n- Movement Speed powerup sprint speed increased from 2 to 4\n- Fire Rate powerup reduced from +20% to +15%\n- Fire Rate powerup duration increased from 25s to 35s\n- Health Regen powerup regen reduced from 1.5% to 1%\n- Health Regen powerup duration increased from 15s to 25s\n- Cooldown Reduction powerup reduced from 25% to 20%\n- Extra Clip powerup reduced from 4 clips to 3\n- Extra Stamina powerup duration increased from 60s to 80s\n- Extra Stamina powerup amount reduced from 2 to 1\n\n- Crouch movespeed increased from 4 to 4.75\n- Bullet Shields now block damage from melee (similar to resistance)\n- Increased the distance heroes slide when they come to the bottom of a ramp\n\n\n\n[ Item Gameplay ]\n\n- Extra Regen: Regen increased from 1.75 to 2\n- Enduring Spirit: Spirit Lifesteal reduced from +10% to +8%\n- Reactive Barrier: Cooldown reduced from 35s to 25s\n- Decay changed from 2.2% of Max Health to 2.4% Current Health (less damage when the target is low, and non-lethal now)\n- Healbane moved from Spirit tree to Vitality tree\n- Healbane now gives +50 Health\n- Spirit Lifesteal: Reduced from +25% to +23%\n- Soul Shredder Bullets: Spirit Amp reduced from +12% to +10%\n- Cold Front: cooldown reduced from 40s to 32s\n- Improved Cooldown: HP Regen reduced from 2 to 1.5\n- Knockdown: No longer provides +2 Sprint\n- Knockdown: Now provides +1 Stamina\n- Shifting Shroud: Cooldown reduced from 45s to 35s\n- Shifting Shroud: Bonus spirit duration increased from 6s to 10s\n- Sharpshooter: Damage falloff bonus reduced from 20% to 15%\n- Metal Skin duration reduced from 4s to 3s\n- Metal Skin cooldown increased from 25s to 30s\n- Intensifying Magazine: Time for Max Damage reduced from 4s to 3.5s\n- Torment Pulse: Interval improved from 2s to 1.5s\n- Torment Pulse: Damage per pulse reduced from 45 to 40 (total dps from 22.5 to 26.6)\n- Torment Pulse: HP bonus reduced from +150 to +125\n- Majestic Leap: Velocity increased from 22.5 to 27\n- Leech: Spirit Lifesteal reduced from +35% to +33%\n- Refresher: No longer has cast time (was 1s)\n- Mystic Reverb: Fixed the damage being reduced by armor twice\n- Mystic Reverb: Damage reduced from 35% to 30%\n- Curse: No longer prevents stamina usage\n- Curse: Now also prevents item usage\n- Curse: Duration reduced from 3.75s to 3.5s\n- Diviner's Kelvar: Bullet Shield increased from 700 to 1000\n- Boundless Spirit: Spirit increased from 45 to 50\n- Spiritual Overflow: Spirit duration increased from 14s to 18s\n\n\n\n[ Hero Gameplay]\n\n- Abrams: Base HP growth reduced from 36 to 32\n- Abrams: Infernal Resilience T2 reduced from +200 Health to +150\n- Abrams: Seismic Impact personal brief busy state upon landing increased from 0.2s to 0.3s\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam slow now affects stamina dashes\n- Dynamo: Base HP growth reduced from 48 to 44\n- Dynamo: Quantum Entanglement fire rate bonus reduced from +30% to +25%\n- Dynamo: Singularity base radius increased from 8m to 10m\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger T3 damage reduction on target improved from -30% to -40%\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb sprint speed now instantly kicks in after casting it\n- Haze: Bullet Dance cooldown reduced from 160s to 130s\n- Haze: Bullet Dance T2 changed to -40s Cooldown\n- Haze: Bullet Dance T3 changed to +1 Targets Hit Per Shot\n- Infernus: Flame Dash cooldown increased from 30s to 38s\n- Ivy: Watcher's Covenant heal sharing reduced from 65% to 55%\n- Kelvin: Ice Path sprint speed increased from +1 to +2\n- Kelvin: Ice Path trail duration increased from 12s to 15s\n- Kelvin: Frozen Shelter T2 changed to -40s Cooldown\n- Kelvin: Frozen Shelter T3 changed to +70 Health Regen that scales with Spirit\n- Kelvin: Frozen Shelter can now be dismissed early\n- McGinnis: Spectral Wall can now be dismissed early\n- Pocket: Barrage channel can now be interrupted by casting your other abilities\n- Pocket: Barrage radius increased from 6m to 6.5m\n- Pocket: Enchanted Satchel cast time reduced from 0.25s to 0.15s\n- Seven: Storm Cloud T1 increased from +30% to +35% Bullet Resist\n- Vindicta: Flight spirit bonus damage reduced from +12 to +10\n- Vindicta: Flight no longer has +10% bonus range to damage falloff\n- Vindicta: Flight cooldown increased from 35s to 50s\n- Vindicta: Flight T2 changed from +12 Spirit Damage to -15s Cooldown\n- Vindicta: Flight T3 changed from +3 m/s Bonus Air Speed to +12 Spirit Damage\n- Vindicta: Can no longer melee while flying for extra movement\n- Vindicta: Assassinate now ramps up its total damage from 50% to 100% over 1 second of being zoomed (UI indication for this)\n- Vindicta: Fixed being unable to move while scoped in with Assassinate\n- Warden: Base ammo increased from 14 to 16\n- Warden: Alchemical Flask T3 increased from +25% Fire Rate Slow to +35%\n- Warden: Willpower T3 increased from +150 Spirit Shield to +200",
````

````json
  "id": "2024-06-13",
  "posted_at": "2024-06-13",
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes]\n\n- Voice/Text chat is now opt-in. There is a prompt pre-match for joining the chat, or you can control it at any time from the ESC menu during a match.\n- You can now press ESC to mute individual players. Muted accounts will not be alerted, and mutes will persist across games.\n- Parties can now set their in-game chat mode to be Party-only, All Team or Chat Disabled (if using discord or other programs)\n\n- Spirit stat has now been renamed to Spirit Power with updated icon\n- Top bar now highlights your lane partner and lane enemies during the laning phase\n- Increased difficulty for hard mode bots\n- Added sandbox option for spawning neutrals\n- Improved the camera when using Dynamo's Quantum Entanglement\n- Added overhead number counter effect for opponents to see the stacks of Berserker\n- Increased the range the enemy-candle-behind-wall indicator shows up from 25m to 35m\n- Fixed ping wheel randomly coming up instantly instead of doing an in-world ping\n- Added ping indicator on the HUD indicating the direction the ping came from (enabled by default in settings)\n- Ping Wheel context slot will now show nothing if there is no context currently\n- Added new UI indicating how many friends you have that are in the game\n- Improved visibility of the edge of Dynamo's Singularity\n\n- Fixed various issues with bots getting stuck\n- Fixed Build descriptions not showing up properly with the in-game tooltip version\n- Moved HUD elements closer to center for ultra wide monitors\n- Added heal and kill events to the contextual ping wheel slot\n- Fixed AP not showing when you have your hero selected\n- Fixed TAB/ALT not bringing up your abilities when spectating the Patron\n- Added indicator for burrow damage radius for the local player\n- Fixed Affliction sometimes becoming lethal when combined with Escalating Exposure\n- Fixed Yamato's Crimson Slash visual direction being wrong sometimes\n- Added Patron VO for when allied or enemy base guardians are destroyed\n- Adjusted the builds drop down in-game to be more clear it can be changed there\n- Fixed Dynamo's Singularity moving the Mid Boss\n- Fixed various bugs with Shifting Shroud\n- Added a new \"major damage\" sound effect to indicate large instances of damage to the local player\n- Fixed Lash's Ground Strike T3 not being accurate in the tooltip\n- Fixed Diviner's Kevlar gaining the Bullet Shield buff if the ultimate was manually cancelled\n- Updated effects for Unstoppable\n- Minor improvements to visibility of Pocket's Affliction cast\n- Updated effects to Alchemical Fire to make it less noisy\n- Reload anims now play correctly in relation to the reload timer. Previously they would end prematurely if reloads were paused during dash/melee/abilities\n- Fixed some bugs with Pocket/channeling\n- A few buildings are 2-3 stories taller\n- Added new effects for Knockdown\n- Added new effects for Vampiric Burst\n- Fixed Mo & Krill gaining Reactive Barrier buff while channeling the ultimate\n- Added instructional text to the bottom of the ping wheel container reminding that they can customize it in settings\n- Changed Ivy's True Form ability name to Stone Form\n- Fixed bugs that were keeping some heroes desperation ability use lines from playing correctly (casting an ability when you're below 10% should play different VO)\n- Fixed several instances of ability use VO getting swallowed up by SFX\n- Boosted volume of ult cast VO\n- Added various ambient sounds - walla, birds, drones - and overall mix tweaks\n- Iterated on binding word sound effects\n- Added Curse item selection start, loop, and end sounds\n- The Patrons no longer care about the well being of the Urn Runner, and so they will stop asking you to protect them (patrons will no longer call out the urn being picked up)\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Added two new underground tunnels, one on each side of the map. The tunnels connect the underground shop to the Bodega area on Amber and the Theater area on Sapphire.\n- Added two new teleporters, one on each outer lane (behind the Chapel and behind the Firehouse)\n\n- Urn reward now has a comeback component where it gives extra gold for the team that is behind based on the magnitude\n- A small share of the comeback portion of hero kill bounty is now given to each other player outside of just the kill/assist players. This also makes the amount the killer gets be more consistent, previously the amount could vary significantly based on how many people were involved. The killer and assist players still get the bigger shares.\n- Kill assist window increased from 7s to 10s\n\n- Rejuvenator is no longer area pick up and now requires someone to heavy melee it to pick it up after it's dropped to the ground\n- Rejuvenator will no longer stop dropping if it touches player created geometry (like Ice Dome) and always goes down to the target position\n\n- You can air-dash downward by pressing CTRL+SHIFT while in the air\n- Successful parry reduces the cooldown from 1s to 0.5s (unsuccessful parry cd is still 6s)\n- Heroes HP growth per boon increased by +5 (there are 11 instances of these). This is not done for Abrams or Dynamo.\n- Post lane medic troopers will heal other troopers if a hero within range is full hp\n- Post lane medic troopers' hero heal increased from 40 to 80\n- Base zipline speed increased by 5%\n- If the Soul Urn is dropped it goes back to its original spot a little bit faster\n- Healing/Spawn area in the base now has its defense damage go up over time\n- Carrying the Soul Urn no longer causes you to get slowed briefly by 50% when you get shot\n- Carrying the Soul Urn now always slows you by 15%\n- Carrying the Soul Urn now also silences your items\n- The silence effect for Soul Urn now applies as you start channeling to pick it up (0.5s after touching it, to prevent accidental walk overs)\n- Base side doors near the healing area now open a lot faster\n\n\n\n[ Item Gameplay ]\n\n- Mystic Burst: No longer grants +3 Spirit Power\n- Mystic Burst: No longer grants +1 Sprint\n- Mystic Burst: Now grants +40 Spirit Shield\n- Mystic Burst: Now grants +6% Weapon Power\n- Headshot Booster: Bonus headshot damage reduced from 50 to 45\n- Medic Bullets: Healing from heroes increased from 25 to 30\n- Extra Health: Health increased from 125 to 135\n- Divine Barrier: No longer grants +2 Sprint\n- Divine Barrier: Bullet Resist reduced from 10% to 7%\n- Divine Barrier: Cooldown increased from 20 to 30\n- Divine Barrier: Active Move Speed reduced from 3 to 2\n- Enchanter's Barrier: Shield increased from 275 to 300\n- Bullet Resist Shredder: Debuff duration increased from 4s to 5s\n- Healbane: Bonus HP increased from 50 to 75\n- Healing Booster: No longer grants +50 HP\n- Healing Booster: Now grants +8% Spirit Resist\n- Swift Striker: Armor penalty reduced from -7% to -6%\n- Fleetfoot: Cooldown increased from 20s to 22s\n- Reactive Barrier: Cooldown reduced from 25s to 20s\n- Cold Front: Time to expand reduced from 1.5s to 0.75s\n- Majestic Leap: Cooldown increased from 15s to 20s\n- Majestic Leap: Drop down speed reduced from 51 to 36\n- Burst Fire: Cooldown reduced from 10s to 9s\n- Rescue Beam: No longer grants movement speed\n- Rescue Beam: Active has been reworked. Now channels for 3 seconds, healing you and the target for up to 25% of Max HP. If you click the ally again during that time, you will pull them to you. Has 40m cast range and 45s cooldown. Can be self cast.\n- Tesla Bullets: Internal cooldown reduced from 0.4s to 0.35s\n- Mystic Slow: Bonus HP increased from +75 to +100\n- Shifting Shroud: Renamed to Ethereal Shift\n- Warp Stone: Fixed some cases where it would fail to cast near objects\n- Escalating Resilience: Stacks share a duration now and each new stack refreshes it\n- Escalating Resilience: Duration reduced from 15s to 10s\n- Escalating Resilience: Max resistance reduced from 36% to 30%\n- Alchemical Fire: Cooldown reduced from 34s to 30s\n- Crippling Headshot: Bullet and Spirit resistance reduction reduced from 35% to 30%\n- Boundless Spirit: Spirit Power increased from +50 to +55\n- Boundless Spirit: Now grants +175 Health\n- Richochet: Ammo increased from +25% to +30%\n- Vampiric Burst: Lifesteal increased from 70% to 80%\n- Refresher: Now has a 0.3s cast time\n\n\n\n[ Hero Gameplay ]\n\n- Abrams: Base health regen reduced from 2 to 1\n- Abrams: Infernal Resilience T1 reduced from +2 HP Regen to +1.5\n- Bebop: Sticky Bomb base damage reduced from 130 to 120\n- Bebop: Hook grab radius reduced from 40 inches to 36\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam movement and dash slow increased from 30% to 40%\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam DPS increased from 210 to 220\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam tracking speed increased by 10%\n- Dynamo: Singularity radius reduced from 10m to 9m\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl cooldown reduced from 130s to 120s\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger damage Spirit growth increased from 2.5 to 2.8\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger T3 is now -12% Bullet Resistance for 6 seconds\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb T1 increased from +4 m/s Invis Sprint Speed to +5\n- Haze: Smoke Bomb causes your footsteps to be silent\n- Ivy: Watcher's Covenant replicated healing reduced from 55% to 45%\n- Ivy: Watcher's Covenant no longer provides +2 m/s in the base ability\n- Ivy: Watcher's Covenant T2 movement bonus increased from +2 m/s to +3\n- Kelvin: Frozen Shelter movement speed slow increased from 25% to 35%\n- Kelvin: Can now dismiss Frozen Shelter while channeling other abilities (Arctic Beam)\n- Lash: Ground Strike base damage reduced from 110 to 90\n- Lash: Ground Strike damage per meter increased from 6 to 6.75\n- Lash: Ground Strike now reduced scaling after 25m height to 4.5\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret charge delay reduced from 10s to 8s\n- McGinnis: Fixed Echo Shard to be compatible with Spectral Wall again (Activating echo shard resets the ability so you can cast it again and not have to cancel your first one. After casting the 2nd, using cancel will remove the first wall first)\n- McGinnis: Can now dismiss Spectral Wall while channeling other abilities (Heavy Barrage)\n- Mo & Krill: HP Growth per boon reduced from 48 to 42\n- Mo & Krill: Combo now grants 25 max hp each time a target dies during it\n- Seven: Storm Cloud initial radius increased from 10m to 12m\n- Seven: Storm Cloud T2 bonus radius reduced from 12m to 10m\n- Yamato: Power Slash T3 bonus damage reduced from +210 to +200\n- Yamato: Shadow Transformation cooldown increased from 100 to 110",
  "title": "06-13-2024 Update",

}
````

````json
  "raw_content": "[ Map Changes ]\n\nMid Lane Rework (between Guardians):\n- Removed the Arch Monument.\n- Removed the rope entrances to Mid Boss.\n- Removed barrels and kiosk low cover.\n- Replaced bridge with a larger version.\n- Added stairway entrance to Mid Boss.\n- Moved the veil areas to fit with the new bridge.\n- Veil areas connect to Mid Boss.\n- Extended the wall cover further toward the bridge.\n\nMid Boss Changes:\n- Started Mid Boss Sewer theming.\n- Added connection from the rope shafts to upper area of Mid Boss.\n- Increase height of entrances to rope shafts from Mid Boss.\n- Added sloped mounds on side of stairs from the pit.\n\n\n- Adjusted breakables that were spread out in the underground\n\n\n\n[ Gameplay Changes ]\n\n- Flex Slot changed from \"Two Guardians\" to \"Three Guardians\"\n- Flex Slot changed from \"Two Walker\" to \"Three Walkers\"\n\n- Guardian and Walker total bounty is now split with all nearby players (including players that have done damage to it within 20s). Nearby players get 40% and the rest is split evenly with all 6 players (including those nearby).\n- Trooper damage to Walkers reduced from 80 DPS to 60 DPS\n\n- Hero Kill bounty increased from 250->1800 to 300->2000 (over 40 minutes, previously over 45 minutes)\n- Killing heroes no longer spawns orbs, instead the souls are directly awarded to the killer and assisters\n- Destroying objectives no longer spawns orbs, instead the souls are directly awarded to the killer and their team\n- Trooper base bounty reduced from 132 to 120\n- Trooper bounty growth per minute increased from 1 to 1.5\n\n- Time holding Urn before taking damage reduced from 90s to 45s (1% max health damage per second)\n- Time Urn will Autorun back to Home regardless of nearby players reduced from 75s to 45s\n- Time an Urn will wait for a nearby player to pick it up reduced from 20s to 12s\n\n- Cleaned up how boons are distributed. Previously some levels gave stat bonuses and some AP/Unlocks, now every level gives one of each. Power rebalanced on abilities and items to be the same overall as before. Spirit Resist reduced from 40% to 30%\n\n- Soul Orb auto claim radius reduced from 40m to 35m\n- Headshot multiplier reduced from 1.9x to 1.8x\n- Fixed some issues with hitting troopers while on ziplines\n- Fix potential prediction issue on guns, causing clients' spread to potentially decay faster\n\n- Rejuv drop duration reduced from 9s to 8s\n\n- Opening Rounds: No longer works against objectives\n- Extra Regen: Regen reduced from 3.5 to 3\n- Healing Booster: Regen reduced from 3.5 to 3\n- Enchanter's Emblem: Spirit Resistance reduced from 18% to 15%\n- Fortitude: Damage taken 30%\n- Rescue Beam: Cooldown increased from 30s to 60s\n- Radiant Regeneration: Regen reduced from 5 HP to 4\n- Radiant Regeneration: Healing on ability cast reduced from 100 to 70\n- Radiant Regeneration: Healing on ability cast now scales with Boons (1.5)\n- Tankbuster only goes on cooldown when it affects players (no longer goes on cooldown if an ability hits a trooper)\n- Superior Cooldown: Cooldown Reduction reduced from 25% to 24%\n\n\n- Calico: Ava no longer automatically breaks boxes, instead you can use the melee hotkey to do a pounce attack to break them\n- Calico: Base Health reduced from 700 to 650\n- Calico: Base Health Growth reduced by 13%\n- Calico: Leaping Slash melee multiplier has been reduced from 1.6 to 1.3\n- Calico: Return to Shadows T3 reduced from +30%   "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added EU servers and matchmaking region. EU testing times are Mon-Thur 5PM to 3AM and Fri-Sun 3PM to 3AM. Times listed are Central European Summer Time (GMT+2).\n\n- Removed the invite requirement of Steam friends having been on your list for a number of days (affected normal user cases often where friends weren't on the Steam list, and we have other metrics and tools on the backend now that we can use to filter for other cases)\n- Added search functionality to the shop. You can now search by item name or by individual stat such as 'Ammo', 'Lifesteal', or 'Spirit Power'.\n- The game will now let you know when it is safe to abandon (usually if one person has been disconnected for a while)\n\n- Your personal builds are now always sorted to the top of the in-game Builds dropdown\n- Various network related improvements when there is packet loss\n- Preferred heroes in your roster are now remember on the client across sessions\n- Fixed chat wheel not working during the post-game sequence\n\n- Updated the entrance to the underground tunnels from the outer lanes to look more like a subway entrance\n- Re-enabled ultra wide UI mode\n- Added street names and symbols to the Zipline UI\n- Updated Sleep Dagger and Silence Glyph tooltips to clarify that they do not cancel channeling abilities\n- Updated reload animations for Warden, Dynamo, and Ivy\n- Improved visibility of Inferno's Concussive Combustion\n- Fixed the unit target UI not showing up for instant-cast abilities (MoKrill ult, Lady Geist ult, Bebop bomb)\n- Fixed the unit target UI not showing the activation button hint\n- Added a Cosmic Veil to the hero demo map\n- Added Ethereal Shift sounds\n- Added wind sound when on the Zipline\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Rejuv buff is now only granted to the players that are alive. Players that are dead will have their respawn times cut in half from their current values.\n\n- Footsteps are now quieter when obstructed by level geometry\n- Dash-Down is now double tap CTRL instead of CTRL+SHIFT\n- Vault bounty increased from 300 to 330\n- Fixed various bugs with Headshot Booster and Sharpshooter\n\n- Added another entrance/exit to the underground tunnels from the warehouse interiors\n- Enclosed the exterior Medium Neutral Camp with fencing in front of the Theater to match the Amber side Cab Stand\n- Added two more veils on each side:\n1) Between the Drug Store and the Market on Amber, between the Hotel and Pharmacy on Sapphire\n2) Between the exterior Strong Neutral Camps and the outer lane near the T2\n\n\n\n[ Item Gameplay ]\n\n- Added a new T1 Spirit item, Infuser\n+8% Spirit Resist\n+10% Ammo\nActive: Gain 12 Spirit and 20% Spirit Lifesteal for 6 seconds. Cosmic Veils regenerate any shields you have. Cooldown: 20s\n\n\n- Added a new T2 Weapon item, Melee Charge\n+15% Melee Damage\n+10% Weapon Power\n+75 Health\nPassive: Hitting an enemy with a heavy melee reloads your weapon\nPassive: Increases your heavy melee charge distance by 40%\n\nInspired by https://project8.dev/threads/bayonet.5372/ and https://project8.dev/threads/comple...-category-item-that-buffs-charged-melee.5957/\n\n\n- Added a new T4 Vitality item, Colossus\n+500 HP\n+20% Melee Damage\n+20% Weapon Damage\nActive: Gain 40% Bullet and Spirit Resist and apply 20% movement slow to enemies within 10m. Cooldown: 65s\n\n\n- Healing Rite: Duration increased from 15s to 17s (total regen increased from 350 to 370)\n- Extra Spirit: Spirit Power increased from +6 to +7\n- Extra Charge: Now grants +7% collision\n- Abrams: Seismic Impact can be held for up to 3 seconds.   "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added a hero based mmr system, so when you join a match with a specific hero the matchmaker adjusts the skill level it assumes you are at by a percentage up or down based on your experience and performance levels with that hero.\n\n- Added settings option to allow weapon zoom to be a toggle\n- Added a game volume slider which will control volume independently from voice chat (VoIP)\n- General improvements to voice chat quality\n- Non-matchmaking lobbies are now considered safe to abandon\n- Crates now show the amount of souls they have in the tooltip\n- Greater contrast between team and enemy sounds\n- Updated headshot hit indicator\n- Updated McGinnis weapon sounds\n- Updated Vindicta weapon sounds\n- Updated Lady Geist weapon sounds\n- Fixed a bug where pinging Bebop's Hook and Sticky Bomb abilities would call the wrong ability name\n- Fixed a recent bug with Metal Skin that caused you to do zero bullet damage\n- Fixed a bug with releasing Grey Talon's Owl early\n- Updated Dynamo's animation while using Rejuvenating Aurora\n- Fixed a bug that could allow Grey Talon to hit the same target multiple times when close during Rain of Arrows\n- Fixed a bug that could cause button input detection to malfunction\n- Fixed an issue where the Rejuvenator being stolen was firing incorrectly\n- Fixed a bug introduced in last week's update that caused gun spreads to not work properly\n- Added an effect at the bottom of a player's screen whenever they are standing on something that causes damage\n- Improved Abrams animation while hovering in the air during his ultimate and adjusted the downward acceleration animation\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Flex slot removed from killing the Patron and added to killing all Walkers\n- Urn now spawns on both sides of the map. Instead they now drop small permanent bonuses. These stack and do not decay with time or death. Drop rates rebalanced.\n\n+2 Spirit\n+1.5% Fire Rate\n+3% Ammo\n+15 Max HP (this has slightly higher odds of dropping than the others)\n+0.5% Cooldown Reduction\n+2% Weapon Damage\n\n\n- At defined locations around the map, powerups now spawn starting 10 minutes (and every 5 minutes after). There are 6 spots where they spawn and they are indicated on the minimap when they are up.\n\n- Movement powerup duration increased from 30s to 50s\n- Fire Rate powerup duration increased from 35s to 45s and +15% Fire Rate to +18%\n- Regeneration powerup duration increased from 25s to 45s\n- Stamina powerup duration increased from 80s to 120s\n- Cooldown powerup is unchanged\n- Clip powerup is unchanged\n\n\n- Breakables initial spawn time increased from 2 min to 3 min\n- Fixed being able to hide inside the Guardian's head\n- Jumping after a dash jump no longer causes your hero to sometimes jump in place\n- Breakables souls base value reduced by 30%. Increases to +15% of current values over 30 minutes. (so -30% to +15% from start to 30 min)\n- All hero guns' reload time reduced by 5%\n- Zipline Boost ability cooldown reduced from 380s to 340s\n- Medic Trooper heal now bounces to up to one other allied hero within 25m from the hero\n\n- Trooper gold sharing radius from the location of the allied hero reduced from 50m to 40m\n- Creep waves now land at their lane destinations at the same time as each other\n\n- Added connection hallway between outer lane Shop and crate room\n- Opened up Authority Building and Jazz Club to the Guardian side\n- Added intermediate cover in front of Amber Orange Guardian and Sapphire Blue Guardian\n- Added Veils to entrances to base fountain\n- Extended cover near Sapphire Orange Guardian to match Amber Blue\n- Extended alcove on outer lane cover position in front of the Shop\n- Underground tunnels have more light\n- Removed underground tunnel alcoves\n- Added signs for entrance to the underground tunnel from the warehouse side\n\n\n\n[ Item Gameplay ]\n\n- Extra Charge: CD reduction increased from +7% to +8%\n- Infuser: Active Spirit Power increased from +12 to +18\n- Ammo Scavenger: Now a Spirit Item\n- Ammo Scavenger: No longer grants +20% Fire Rate for 4s on Soul Secure/Deny\n- Ammo Scavenger: Now grants +3 Spirit for 18s on Soul Secure/Deny. Multiple instances stack and refresh the duration.
````
