````json
  "id": "2024-07-04",
  "posted_at": "2024-07-04",
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added Asia servers and matchmaking region. Testing times for this region are Mon-Thur 5PM to 3AM and Fri-Sun 3PM to 3AM in China Standard Time (GMT+8).\n\n- Added South America servers and matchmaking region. Testing times for this region are Mon-Thur 5PM to 3AM and Fri-Sun 3PM to 3AM in Atlantic Standard Time (GMT-4).\n\n- Added a new Damage Report feature. You can now open a flyout panel within the shop to view a breakdown of your hero's incoming and outgoing damage throughout the course of a match. You can also hover an enemy hero to view a quick breakdown of their key stats and items. The data can be filtered by last respawn, 10 minutes, 5 minutes, 3 minutes, 1 minute, or 30 seconds.\n\n- Reworked the top bar HUD to reduce overall height\n\n- While spectating or watching replays, the game now has a free cursor mode without needing to hold modifiers down (defaulted to on). Can toggle it off while spectating using the 'J' key.\n- Minimap now displays dead heroes for 5 seconds after their death\n\n- Reworked the default suggested items for heroes to be much more simple (oriented more strongly for new player onboarding)\n\n- Parties will now be a little more likely to be in the same lane, if there isn't a wide skill gap or other factors preventing it\n- All instances of collecting souls (whether claiming your own or denying an opponent) will share the same sound. Being denied a soul orb by an enemy will have its own unique sound.\n- Fixed the HUD cooldown state thrashing while picking up the Soul Urn\n- Fixed Spectral Wall sometimes not spawning depending on the map geometry nearby\n- Added descriptive tooltips to Render and Audio options within settings. Render tooltips include an estimate of performance impact where applicable.\n- Fixed a bug where bullets could penetrate close range targets and hit 2 targets when not intended (was primarily obvious with neutral farming)\n- Reduced the end of match delay from 10s to 4s\n- Added a shatter sound effect for meleeing the mid boss rejuvenator\n- Added ghost models inside the doorways of the juke closets to help clarify which are dead ends\n- Fixed position of the \"All Walkers Destroyed\" Flex slot to be the 2nd slot rather than the 4th\n- Added a sound for when Infernus's Afterburn procs\n- Added rejuvenator pickup sound\n- Minimap now highlights Soul Urn dropoff location\n- Updated Wraith Full Auto cast sound\n- Added audio feedback for environmental damage\n- Fixed sounds associated with last hit playing unreliably\n- Updated sounds for Wraith Card Trick projectile, impact, and when you gain a card\n- Added traffic and construction ambient sounds and updated general ambience mix\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay]\n\n- Urn count changed from 2 to 1 (previous souls value restored)\n- Urn pickup location moved to closer to the outer lanes (on the opposite side of the lane pillar, facing towards the bridge)\n- Urn drop-off location now move to the same spot as the pickup (opposite end of the map, depending on which location the Urn spawns in)\n- Urn pickup time reduced from 4s to 2s\n- Urn now has a 2s drop-off time\n- Urn no longer applies a 15% slow\n- Urn now prevents you from using the zipline while carrying it\n- The person delivering the urn now gets +25% soul bonus\n\n- Reworked how Powerups work. There are now only 2 locations where they spawn instead of 6 (the two at the strong camps near the middle far edges of the map). The different powerup types are reduced from 6 to 4 and they have been reworked:\nGun: +30% Clip and +20% Fire Rate for 100s\nSurvival: +400 Max HP and +1% HP Regen for 100s\nCasting: +20% Cooldown Reduction and +25 Spirit for 100s\nMovement: +2 Stamina, +2 Sprint and +30% zipline boost for 100s\n- Powerups now always cycle which ones spawn until they have each spawned, and then they are randomly allocated again\n\n- Base Guardians and Shrines now have +40% Bullet Resistance. They lose 20% for each enemy hero nearby (down to a minimum of 0% bullet resistance). When they haven't taken damage for 60 seconds, they regenerate 4% max hp per second.\n- Fixed Walkers not using their stomp properly\n\n- Troopers now deal 25% less damage to other troopers\n- Troopers spawn rate before 10 minutes is now the same as after 10 minutes (30s->25s)\n\n- Breakables are no longer pre-placed on the map, they now spawn in when they are able to roll for drops (3 minutes)\n- Breakables now use pseudorandom rolls (makes streaks of success or failure less likely)\n- Golden Statue bonuses now have some dynamic weighting based on what has already dropped (makes it less likely that you have too many of one specific bonus over the course of the game)\n- Golden Statue's CD Reduction bonus increased from 0.5% to 0.75%\n- After 25 minutes, the Golden Statue bonuses that drop are slightly stronger:\nMax HP: +15 to +20\nWeapon Damage: +2% to +3%\nSpirit Power: +2 to +3\nFire Rate: +1.5% to +2%\nCD Reduction: 0.75% to 1%\nAmmo: 3% to 5%\n\n- Bullet falloff damage   "raw_content": "[ General ]\n\n- Mid Boss HP is now fixed at the spawn time. Base HP increased from 6725 to 12500 and growth reduced from 470 to 200.\n- Reworked Rejuv reward from Mid Boss. No longer buffs troopers, reduces respawn time and buffs various stats. Upon death, 3 rejuv crystals fall to the ground (4 for subsequent midboss kills). Claiming one now grants you in place revive after 3s. If you already claimed one and claim another, it'll go to an ally (priority is closest allies first, then most recent dead allies). Dying with Rejuv no longer counts as a kill/death nor gives out bounties. While you have the Rejuv buff, you gain the following: +20% Max HP, +20% Fire Rate, and +15% Spirit Damage. Gaining the buff heals you to full and lasts up to 7 minutes.\n\n\n- Meleeing a mantling enemy no longer cancels their mantle, instead it applies 80% movement slow that ramps down to 20% over 2s.\n- Adding a debuff that makes you climb ropes 40% slower for 4s after you've taken player damage. This debuff only exists when you're on a rope, and tapers off to 0% in the last 1s of the damage window.\n\n\n- Slight reduction to the amount of turning you can do while performing a heavy melee\n- Light melee base damage reduced by 20% (except for Viscous, Calico and Bebop)\n- Heavy Melee cooldown increased from 0.9s to 1.0s\n- Heavy Melee cooldown when missing increased from 0.9s to 1.3s\n- Parried stun duration is no longer reduced by debuff resistance\n- Parry bonus damage is no longer melee specific, now affects all damage types (reduced from 35% to 30%)\n\n\n- Heroes now have different dash speeds (same distance, slower to get there). They are placed into 3 buckets to start, Bucket 1: +5%, Bucket 2: -5%, Bucket 3: -15%.\n\nBucket 1: Ivy, Haze, Holliday, Calico, Grey Talon, Paradox\nBucket 2: Seven, Mirage, Lash, Yamato, Pocket, Viscous, Vyper, Warden, Vindicta, Sinclair, Wraith, Infernus, Lady Geist\nBucket 3: Mo & Krill, Abrams, Shiv, Dynamo, Bebop, Kelvin, McGinnis\n\n\n- Removed the teleporters behind the Walkers\n- After 20 minutes, trooper spawn time is reduced from 30s to 25s\n- Trooper bounty growth reduced from 1.5 to 1.2\n- Removed the bounce pads on top of the Nursery/Fish Market rooftop leading back to Mid.\n\n\n- Trooper ally split updated from 100/65/30/20/15/11% to 100/65/28/15/12/8%\n- Breakables souls growth per minute reduced from 2.6 to 2.0\n- Reduced comeback multipliers a bit\n- Neutrals bounty increased by 15% (strong neutrals +25%)\n- Medium Neutrals respawn rate increased from 360s to 420s (+17%)\n- Strong Neutrals respawn rate increased from 480s to 600s (+25%)\n\n- Sinner's Sacrifice: Retaliate damage increased from 40 to 60\n- Sinner's Sacrifice: Heavy melee end success bonus now grants 4 buffs rather than 3 (these also take a little longer to kill now due to the melee timing changes)\n\n- Hero bounties are now reduced based on the amount of players, similar to the trooper mechanic.   "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added a new hero, Viscous. He's green.\n1722549138302.png\n\n\n- Improved client performance\n\n- Reworked the Hero Builds system. Browsing and editing builds now happens within the Shop UI (accessible via Sandbox or during a normal game). You can create custom categories for items with unique names and descriptions that can be independently resized and positioned. The interface for browsing builds has also been improved, public builds will now be sorted by popularity (number of favorites). Old builds are incompatible with this system, so they won't be visible in this UI, but we'll have a console command we'll release soon that you can use to print out your older guides if you need them for reference.\n\n\n\n- North America (NA) servers have been moved from the west coast to closer to the middle of the country to make latency between east and west coast a little more even, as the majority of users are east coast now. The total average ping amongst all players will be lower as a result of this (a little higher for some and a lot lower for others).\n- NA region testing times now start 1 hour earlier weekdays and weekends\n\n- Minimum hero roster requirement reduced from 4 to 3\n\n- Added Oceanic servers and matchmaking region. Testing times are displayed in the client\n\n- Added Teleporter icons on the minimap. The icons glow when you get close to them and pulse on the teleporter's exit location to show you the destination when you are about to enter it.\n\n\n\n- When using the underground tunnels, the primary minimap fades out and an underground map for that area shows you your path and the exit locations.\n\n\n\n- Reworked the invite system in the client to streamline the process. Emails are no longer required, the users will now receive access directly through Steam and Discord/Forum access will be available through the client. The UI will now also group users into three categories: available to invite, already invited, and already have access. This new system will result in less invitations stuck in limbo, and any previous invites that have not already claimed the game will be granted through this system as well.\n\n- Hero Sandbox: Added an orb spawner for practicing last-hitting orbs\n- Hero Sandbox: Added a dummy hero that patrols left and right to practice targeting\n\n- Added a checkbox on the startup development dialog to be able to choose to not show it again\n\n- Your vision through a Cosmic Veil is now based on your hero position rather than camera position\n- Adjusted Zipline camera positions for various heroes to help with visibility\n- Double tap threshold increased from 0.2s to 0.3s\n- Fixed Candle Troopers near-death flash sometimes occurring while moving or doing other active animations\n- Candle Trooper behind-wall HUD indicator now also shows near-death state\n\n- Fixed taking damage not interrupting teleporters\n- Fixed clicking on the top bar or minimap to change spectate targets being unreliable\n- Fixed Function keys (F1-F5) being broken recently and not spectating team mates\n- Fixed various abilities not showing the cast preview correctly on the first use (Mini Turrets, Quantum Entanglement, etc)\n- Fixed Paradox Swap sometimes putting players behind walls\n- Fixed Lash's Ground Strike sometimes causing the hero to be stuck for the rest of the match\n- Fixed Abrams' Charge and Siphon pings not playing the correct VO\n- Fixed Walkers having a pause at the end of their Rocket Barrage\n- Fixed multiple instances of \"Killing Blow\" in the Damage Report\n- Temporarily disabled the audio reminder on being unable to damage the patron while the shrines are alive\n- Fixed Spectral Wall forcing itself into the first ability slot if its cooldown is refreshed\n- Changed camera to more closely track the followed hero angles when watching replays\n- Player names no longer show up above hero's heads when in replay free-cam mode\n- Increased post-game time by 1s\n- Fixed upgrading abilities via hotkeys not working while dead\n- Fixed dead player minimap icons showing over enemy players who are below street level and over shop icons\n- Fixed various bugs with Infernus that caused significant lag\n- Fixed Shrines not having collision\n- Fixed crosshair sometimes being stuck hidden for Yamato after Flying Strike was interrupted\n- Added a bunch of new Ivy lines including updated pings, kill lines, etc\n- Improved mix clarity for game dialog\n- Improved distinction between orb collect and orb deny sounds\n- Fixed various cheat/debug commands being accessible\n- Updated Pocket animations: New crouch walk and aim, updated slide animation, and new firing from slide animation.\n- Updated Mo & Krill Burrow start, duration, and end sounds\n- Updated Mo & Krill Combo sound\n- Updated Mo & Krill Sand Blast cast sound mix\n- Updated Warden Alchemical Flask impact sounds\n- Updated Warden Binding Word sound mix for teammates\n- Updated Pocket Enchanted Satchel cast and delay sounds\n- Added sleep status sound\n- Updated Wraith zipline animations\n- Fixed a bug with Urn that could cause you to be permanently silenced\n- Fixed shop close sounds playing whenever the player enters the radius of a closed shop\n- Added new visual effects for Lash's Ground Strike\n- Improved ground impact effects for Ivy's True Form\n- The client now culls more sounds when they are below a volume threshold\n- The server no longer tells clients to play certain sounds when players and spectators are outside of audible range\n- Shortened weapons and abilities volume attenuation over distance\n- Fixed the console command zoom_sensitivity_ratio affecting both zoomed in and out\n- Replaced two oil trucks with a new vehicle prop\n- Improved visibility of low health state on troopers about to die\n- Fixed Pocket's Cloak being able to tunnel through some ceilings and out of the world\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Reworked the location of the Walker on Amber Yellow and Sapphire Purple lanes. They are a little further towards the map edges. Nearby terrain has been reworked as well.\nDL_Yellow_changes.png\n\n\n\n- The shooting experience near corners has been improved. Previously if your hero's eyes were obstructed at all, you would be unable to shoot altogether even if a large portion of your body was clearly visible. The check will now be a little more sophisticated and custom per hero, to allow you to shoot if your body is reasonably exposed. A part of this will also involve your hero leaning out a bit if not enough of your model is exposed to enemies.\n\n- Soul Orbs now fly out in different initial directions before floating up. There is a 1/3rd chance it goes left, 1/3rd chance it goes right, and 1/3rd chance it goes up.\n\n\n- Teleporters are now in pairs that send you between them to traverse the map horizontally\n- Removed outer lane teleporters\n- Added teleporters to areas between lanes (one in each quadrant). Power scaling increased from 0.4 to 0.5\n\n- Grey Talon: Charged Shot collision size reduced by 10%\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl explosion radius increased from 10m to 12m\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl cooldown reduced from 120s to 110s\n\n- Haze: Bullet damage falloff is now 20% more falloff than default\n- Haze: Can now cast Smoke Bomb while on zipline\n\n- Infernus: Fixed Infernus being unable to slide out of Flame Dash\n- Infernus: Afterburn T1 and T2 Swapped\n- Infernus: Afterburn base DPS reduced from 18 to 15\n- Infernus: Afterburn T3 DPS reduced from 33 to 30\n- Infernus: Afterburn spirit scaling increased from 0.4 to 0.5\n\n- Kelvin: Fixed Arctic Beam not having proper lag compensation\n- Kelvin: Fixed some cases where Frost Grenade would explode and not hit anyone\n- Kelvin: Arctic Beam can now be canceled directly by using parry\n- Kelvin: Frost Grenade cast time reduced from 0.2s to 0.1s\n- Kelvin: Frost Grenade recharge time reduced from 8s to 7s\n- Kelvin: Ice Path sprint is now granted immediately on use\n\n- Lady Geist: Base sprint reduced from 2 to 1\n\n- Lash: Base sprint reduced from 3 to 1\n- Lash: Ground Strike Damage Per Meter reduced from 6.3 to 6.0\n\n- McGinnis: Mini Turrets no longer target immune structures\n- McGinnis: Heavy Barrage cast/aim behavior improved for indoors and close range\n- McGinnis: Reverted recent melee damage reduction\n- McGinnis: Fixed Health Nova not working on Mini Turrets (when Health Nova was cast by McGinnis)\n- McGinnis: Mini Turret base HP increased from +50 to +75\n\n- Mo & Krill: Can now reload while burrowed\n\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine Time Slowed targets that were moving before the time slow will now resume their momentum when time slow wears off\n- Paradox: Bullet (and Kinetic Carbine) projectile speed reduced by 10%\n- Paradox: Ammo count reduced from 54 to 45\n\n- Pocket: Affliction damage is dealt over 18s instead of 16s (same total damage, lower DPS)\n- Pocket: Enchanter's Satchel T3 changed from Disarms for 2.5s to Applies -40% Fire Rate for 4s\n\n- Seven: Static Charge stun duration reduced from 1.25s to 1.1s\n- Seven: Static Charge T3 stun duration reduced from 1.25s to 1.1s\n- Seven: Static Charge T2 radius increased from +5m to +7m\n- Seven: Headshot damage taken reduced by 25%\n\n- Vindicta: Flight cooldown reduced from 45 to 40\n- Vindicta: Gun damage growth increased from 0.85 to 1.0\n\n- Wraith: Card Trick post cast time reduced from 0.5s to 0.2s\n- Wraith: Fixed some issues Card Trick tracking that could cause it to orb players or go to the wrong spot\n\n- Yamato: Power Slash collision size reduced by 10%\n- Yamato: Bullet damage growth increased from 0.4 to 0.5",
````

````json
  "posted_at": "2025-08-08",
  "raw_content": "- Rejuv now drops 1 crystal again, with each heavy melee hit granting a credit to the team (up to 3 for first midboss, and 4 for subsequent ones). Everyone on the team gains the buff. Whenever an ally dies, they respawn in place, lose the buff and your team rejuv credits get reduced by 1 (an ally can only consume one rejuv credit). The top bar has a UI indicator for how many rejuv credits remain. When all rejuv credits are used, the buff disappears for everyone.\n- Buff duration reduced to 4 minutes (was 7 min in the last rework, and 5 min in the most recent adjustment)\n- Buff reduced from +20% max hp to +15%\n- Buff reduced from +20% fire rate to +15%\n\n- Dash speed for bucket 3 heroes increased from -15% to -10%\n\n- Seven: Base Sprint speed reduced from 2.0 to 1.0\n- Seven: Base regen reduced from 1.5 to 1.0\n- Seven: Base health reduced from 700 to 650\n- Shiv: Bloodletting cooldown reduced from 45s to 25s\n- Shiv: Bloodletting T1 cooldown increased from -25s to -10s\n- Shiv: Bloodletting T2 increased from +6% Deferred to +10%\n- Shiv: Bloodletting Rage deferred bonus from +14% to +15%\n- Shiv: Bloodletting deferred damage from +20% to +25%\n- Vyper: Gun cycle time increased from 0.063 to 0.07 (a nerf)\n- Vyper: Slither T1 slide distance reduced from +25% to +20%\n- Vyper: Headshot Crit value reduced from 80% to 70%\n- Vyper: Fixed some issues with hitbox and its size in some areas",
  "title": "08-08-2025 Update",
  "url": "https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/07-29-2025-update.72760/post-141214"
}
````

````json
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added a new hero, Shiv\n\n- Games can now be watched by an unlimited number of spectators\n- Spectator games now have 1 minute delay\n\n- Added localization for Simplified Chinese, Russian, Brazilian Portuguese, Spanish (Spain), Spanish (Latin America), Turkish, Ukrainian, Czech, and French. These are currently a work-in-progress.\n- Language can be selected from the settings menu\n\n- Matchmaking windows are now open 1 hour earlier in all regions\n\n- You can now prioritize your roster into three groups to indicate your preference ordering to the matchmaker: Selected, Priority, High Priority\n\n- Added customization options for your crosshair\n\n- Builds: You can now author a suggested ordering of Ability Upgrades\n- Builds: You can now add per-item author comments by right clicking on items while editing builds that will show up in the item tooltip for users of the build\n- Fixed a bug that allowed published build data to overwrite local changes to a published build\n- Fixed a bug that prevented the last used hero build from loading when it was not a favorited build\n- Hovering items in the shop will now highlight their components, or which item they are components of\n\n- Reworked the Hero Stats UI in the shop\n- Hero Stats in the shop have been expanded and are now broken up by Weapon, Vitality, and Spirit. Various additional stats that were previously hidden have been added here.\n- Individual stats can now be hovered over to see a breakdown of all the bonuses contributing to a value, including things like golden statues\n- Added descriptive gun information to each hero\n- Added Spirit Power Impact panel that displays the effect of Spirit Power on your Abilities and Items\n\n- Added Outgoing Damage as a UI display mode that you can toggle through\n- Added a setting to let you customize the ability cancel hotkey (default is spacebar)\n- Added a \"Friends Only\" checkbox to filter the watch tab\n- Added an option to control zoom sensitivity\n\n- Fixed Candle Troopers sometimes instantly disappearing on death rather than going into their death flash state\n- Using an active item or ability removes the undo option for the item/AP\n- Fixed some abuse cases with undoing AP cooldown reductions\n- Added a video setting to reduce flashing effects\n- Unpause hotkey is now displayed when the game is paused\n- Pause cooldown per player increased from 10 minutes to 15 minutes\n\n- Improved visual clarity of Grey Talon's trap armed state\n- Replaced the Urn drop parachute with new effects\n- Fixed some cases where the comeback souls would be higher than intended in the early game\n- Fixed being unable to ping while dead\n- Fixed Bebop's Sticky Bomb sometimes misfiring\n- Improved smoothing of spectator camera position and angles\n- Fixed a bug that allowed teleporters to be used while carrying the Soul Urn\n- Fixed targeting previews that draw a model (like McGinnis's turrets) to update every frame, instead of being fixed at 60hz\n- Fixed stuns resetting their duration if you were on the ground and became airborne\n- Fixed some cases where Lash's Ground Strike would cause your hero to be stuck\n- Fixed Ricochet sometimes shooting the extra bullet from your gun rather than the target\n- Fixed Arctic Beam T3 range not being calculated correctly when you have range increase items\n- Fixed ESC not working to close Settings popup sometimes\n- Various visual adjustments on highlight and strobe effects being too loud in some cases\n- Holding M1 down while Flame Dash ends will now cause you to fire your gun instead of requiring you to let go and press again\n- Added popup to bot games to indicate that they tend to be much longer games than normal ones\n- Improved Abrams Siphon and Seismic Impact visuals to not obstruct player view as much\n- Added new Silence status effect sound\n- Updated bullet whizby sounds for nearly all characters\n- Updated Haze Sleep Dagger cast, projectile loop and whizby\n- Updated Haze Smoke Bomb sound\n- Removed Haze Smoke Bomb voice line for everyone except the casting player and spectators\n- Fixed some spectator/replay sound and music bugs\n- Various sound related performance improvements\n- More impactful damage indication sound for attackers\n- Remastered Abrams, Pocket, and Dynamo weapon fire sounds\n- Improved lighting performance around shop\n- Improved Wraith tracers to feel more connected to their bullet\n- Improved visibility of AOE around Bebop while casting Hyper Beam\n- Improved effects around Lady Geist Soul Exchange to help with clarity\n- Updated Infernus tracer, muzzle, and impact fx revisions for better performance\n- Fixed Mimic options in sandbox not working properly\n- Added a sound for movement being restricted by Slowing Hex and Vindicta tether\n- Revised sounds for Pocket Flying Cloak duration and warp\n- Clarified Vindicta Flight T1 tooltip\n- Added alternate shop music\n- Added Refresher cast sound\n\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Teleporter activation no longer gets canceled on incoming damage.\n- Teleporter channel time increased from 4s to 5s\n\n- Walkers now require you to be within 30m to deal damage to them\n- Walker Health increased by 15%\n- Base Guardian Health increased by 20%\n- Patron damage increased by 30% after it is vulnerable\n- Weakened Patron Health increased by 15%\n- Weakened Patron now has a Torment Pulse style effect, hitting up to 2 enemies in the pit for 75 damage per second\n- You can now use melee hotkey while zoomed to exit zoom and perform a melee\n\n- Added Bounce Pads in various areas of the map that launch you in a defined direction\nbounce_pad_locations.jpg\n\n\n\n- Added one-way defender exits beside each outer lane entrance\n- Added Speed Boost Volumes in the defender passages on the outsides of the base\nbase_speed_boost.jpg\n\n\n\n- Firehouse (building next to Sapphire Yellow Guardian) has been reworked to match the layout of the Chapel\n\n- Low roof buildings beside Amber Purple Guardian and Sapphire Yellow Guardian are now valid play spaces\n- Removed zap triggers\n- Added truck on the back side of those buildings to allow climbing up\n- Removed rooftop props for those buildings\n- Added billboards to the edge closest to the lane\n\n- Low roof buildings between inner lane Walker arenas are now valid play spaces\n- Removed zap triggers\n- Expanded rooftop size\n- Added fire escape stairs to allow access from the upper level from the Mid\nrooftop_locations_large.jpg\n\n\n\n\n- Walker Arena - Amber Yellow/Sapphire Purple:\n- Added catwalks from high ground defender area to building across the street\n- Added passage through building and stairs up to a peek spot toward the Guardian\n- Replaced upper floor juke spot with a connection through building across from new peek spot\namber_yellow_walker_arena.jpg\n\n\n\n\n- Walker Arena - Amber Orange/Sapphire Blue:\n- Added catwalk from the low roof building to the building across the street\n- Replaced juke spot in the courtyard with passage connecting to the new catwalk\n- Widened hallway above Underground Shop\n- Removed railing Underground Shop to the upper courtyard. Replaces a juke spot.\namber_orange_walker_arena.jpg\n\n\n\n- Walker Arena - Amber Blue/Sapphire Orange:\n- Added interior shaft with Bounce Pad to an upper level balcony from defender side to face the Guardian\n- Added a Bounce Pad on the ground floor of the Police Station/Garment Building to the upper floor\n- Added a juke spot inside the Police Station/Garment Building\n- Added a juke spot down the stairs from the Guardian to the left\namber_blue_walker_arena.jpg\n\n\n\n\n- Walker Arena - Amber Purple/Sapphire Yellow:\n- Added an interior corridor through the building between the base and the Walker\n- Added a balcony that borders the arena on the back side of the Walker\namber_purple_walker_arena.jpg\n\n\n\n\n[ Weapon Items ]\n\n- Headshot Booster: No longer has +40 Bonus Health\n- Headshot Booster: Now has +40 Bullet Shield\n- Monster Rounds: Health reduced from +65 to +50\n- Monster Rounds: Now grants +30% Bullet Resistance vs NPCs\n- Restorative Shot: Weapon Damage increased from 6% to 8%\n\n- Active Reload: Lifesteal reduced from 40% to 30%\n- Active Reload: Fire Rate reduced from 24% to 20%\n- Kinetic Dash: HP regen increased from 1 to 1.5\n- Long Range: Min distance reduced from 18m to 15m\n- Mystic Shot: Cooldown increased from 5s to 6s\n- Slowing Bullets: Now grants +5 Spirit\n- Soul Shredder Bullets: Now grants +6% Weapon Damage\n- Soul Shredder Bullets: Debuff duration increased from 5s to 6s\n- Berserker: Fixed gaining stacks much faster than intended when taking damage from troopers\n\n- Warp Stone: Active Bullet Resist reduced from +60% to +40%\n- Warp Stone: No longer grants +2 Sprint\n- Warp Stone: Now gains +8 Spirit\n- Point Blank: No longer grants +300 Bullet Shield\n- Point Blank: Now grants +15% Bullet Armor\n- Escalating Resilience: Max Bullet Resist increased from 30% to 36%\n- Heroic Aura: Lifesteal increased from 18% to 20%\n- Alchemical Fire: Bullet Shield increased from 175 to 225\n- Sharpshooter: Min distance reduced from 18m to 15m\n- Titanic Magazine: Weapon Damage increased from 16% to 18%\n- Titanic Magazine: Bullet Resist increased from 8% to 10%\n- Intensifying Magazine: Time for Max Damage reduced from 3.5s to 3s (this is a buff)\n\n- Ricochet: Fire Rate increased from +8% to +12%\n- Ricochet: Health increased from +125 to +150\n- Siphon Bullets: You permanently steal 1 stack (45 HP) from the enemy if they die under the debuff. If an enemy you stole a stack from kills you, they regain 1 stack back.\n- Silencer: Reduced active duration from 6s to 4s\n- Glass Cannon: Now gives +1 Movement Speed\n- Glass Cannon: No longer gives +2 ammo on kill\n- Glass Cannon: Weapon Damage reduced from +75% to +70%\n- Glass Cannon: Now gives +10% Weapon Damage per kill (up to +50%)\n- Glass Cannon: When you die you lose a stack\n\n\n\n\n[ Vitality Items ]\n\n- Melee Lifesteal: Heal effectiveness vs non-heroes increased from 25% to 35%\n- Melee Lifesteal: Health increased from +70 to +75\n- Extra Regen: Regen increased from 2.7 to 2.8\n- Healing Rite: Cooldown reduced from 75s to 65s\n- Healing Rite: Cast Range increased from 20m to 30m\n\n- Healing Booster: Spirit Resist reduced from +7% to +6%\n- Healing Booster: Bullet Superior Stamina: Fire Rate reduced from 10% to 7%\n- Debuff Remover: Debuff resistance reduced from +40% to +35%\n- Lifestrike: Heal effectiveness vs non-heroes increased from 25% to 35%\n- damage increased from +30 to +50\n\n- Improved Cooldown: Cooldown reduction increased from 14% to 15%\n- Ethereal Shift: No longer provides +10% Fire Rate\n- Ethereal Shift: Cooldown increased from 25s to 30s\n- Improved Burst: Reduced Weapon Damage from +12% to +8%\n- Surge of Power: Movespeed duration increased from 5s to 6s\n- Surge of Power: Imbued Spirit increased from +26 to +28\n- Surge of Power: Now grants +75 Health\n- Improved Reach: Now gives +10% Spirit Resist\n- Superior Duration: Now provides +15% Weapon Damage\n- Mystic Slow: Fire Rate reduction increased from -30% to -40%\n- Silence Glyph: No longer reduces Spirit Resist\n- Silence Glyph: Now deals +150 damage on impact\n- Superior Cooldown: Non-Imbued ability cd reduction increased from 22% to 24%\n\n- Escalating Exposure: Max Interval improved from 1s to 0.7s\n- Diviner's Kevlar: Cooldown increased from 30s to 60s\n- Refresher: Spirit Resist increased from 14% to 16%\n- Curse: Cooldown increased from 40s to 50s\n\n\n\n\n[ New Items ]\n\n- Added a new T1 Weapon item. Grants +35% Bullet Projectile Speed, +14% Weapon Damage and +50 Bullet Shield.\n\nInspired by: https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/projectile-speed-increase-item.1539/\n\n\n- Added a new T4 Spirit item, Magic Carpet: Active - After a 1.3s delay, a magic carpet arrives to pick you up. You gain full flight controls and have +6 m/s movement speed. Lasts 7 seconds. If you cast abilities or use your gun, the carpet disappears. While on the Magic Carpet you gain +400 Bullet and +400 Spirit Shields. Passively grants +150 Health, +10% Ability Duration, and +15 Spirit Power. Cooldown: 40s\n\nInspired by: https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/a-bunch-of-loose-item-ideas.11618/\n\n\n\n\n[ Hero Gameplay ]\n\n- Abrams: Fixed Seismic Impact often not dragging enemies in the air along with you when you are slamming down\n- Abrams: Fixed Shoulder Charge not always placing enemies in front of you\n- Abrams: Seismic Impact damage reduced from 175 to 150\n\n- Bebop: Gun range reduced from 50m to 30m + 2.5m/boon (max of 58m)\n- Bebop: Speed while shooting increased from 55% to 70%\n- Bebop: Base regen increased from 2.5 to 3\n- Bebop: Base Bullet Resist increased from 0% to 10%\n- Bebop: Bullet Resist per boon reduced from 2.5% to 1.6% (same overall bullet resist at max)\n- Bebop: Hook range reduced from 40m to 35m\n- Bebop: Hook T2 increased from +25m to +30m\n- Bebop: Exploding Uppercut now has a slight angle limitation to prevent straight-up direction\n- Bebop: Exploding Uppercut radius increased from 10m to 14m\n- Bebop: Exploding Uppercut T1 increased from -10s Cooldown to -11s\n- Bebop: Fixed hooking through veils sometimes not working\n- Bebop: Sticky Bomb radius increased from 10m to 12m\n- Bebop: Sticky Bomb can now be cast on self using alt cast hotkey\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam T2 reduced from +140 to +100\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam spirit scaling increased from 2.4 to 3.0\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam turn rate improved by 10%\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam nearby radius increased from 4.5m to 5m\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam nearby damage now does 25% less than the full amount\n\n- Dynamo: Rejuvenating Aurora can now be canceled by using stamina instead of needing to manually exit first (pre T3)\n- Dynamo: Fixed Kinetic Pulse sometimes getting caught on stairs\n- Dynamo: Singularity now has a 0.1s cast point\n- Dynamo: Kinetic Pulse width increased from 4.5m to 5m\n- Dynamo: Kinetic Pulse T3 increased from +115 Damage to +125\n\n- Grey Talon: Charged Shot collision radius reduced by 10%\n- Grey Talon: Fixed T3 Guided Owl not triggering correctly sometimes when the target has Spirit Armor\n\n- Haze: Sleep Dagger now puts you in a brief drowsy state for 0.25s seconds where you move slowly before falling asleep (you still have the ability to shoot/cast/move/etc during this period)\n- Haze: Fixation T2 from +6s Duration to +5s\n- Haze: Fixation T3 reduced from +0.25 to +0.2\n- Haze: Bullet Dance T1 increased from +6 to +7\n\n- Ivy: Fixed melee still going through when casting Stone Form after initiating a melee attack\n- Ivy: Stone Form T2 stun reduced from 0.75s to 0.5s\n- Ivy: Watcher's Covenant T1 reduced from +15% Fire Rate to +10%\n- Ivy: Now has a new ultimate ability, Air Drop. Take flight with an Ally (or self-cast to carry a bomb). You fly and drop the ally or bomb onto an area, dealing damage, slowing, and silencing enemies. Ivy and the ally gain a bullet shield when they are dropped. Allies can drop by themselves at any time. When using the ability without an ally (carrying a bomb), it has a 1 second cast time.\n\n- Kelvin: Arctic Beam no longer applies a Disarm\n- Kelvin: Arctic Beam now slows Fire Rate (same values as slow)\n- Kelvin: Arctic Beam max slow reduced from 90% to 60%\n- Kelvin: Arctic Beam time to reach max slow increased from 1.2s to 2s\n- Kelvin: Frozen Shelter can now be cast while using Arctic Beam\n\n- Lash: Health per boon increased from 37 to 42\n- Lash: Base spirit resist increased from 8% to 15%\n- Lash: Flog cooldown reduced from 30s to 25s\n- Lash: Flog T3 now also reduces Fire Rate by 35%\n\n- McGinnis: Speed while shooting increased from 55% to 70%\n- McGinnis: Mini Turrets now prioritize any heroes McGinnis is shooting at\n- McGinnis: Medicinal Specter duration Spirit Power scaling increased from 0.09 to 0.12\n- McGinnis: Heavy Barrage T1 changed to Rockets apply 35% slow\n\n- Mo & Krill: Scorn T3 now affects all damage types\n\n- Paradox: Base ammo reduced from 45 to 40\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine projectile size reduced by 10%\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine cooldown increased from 24s to 28s\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine speed duration reduced from 4s to 3.5s\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine no longer affects channels, cast times or things like Lash's Death Slam\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine T1 reduced from +0.75s Max Stop duration to +0.5s\n- Paradox: Kinetic Carbine T2 cd reduction increased from -8s to -10s\n- Paradox: Time Wall T3 silence duration reduced from 3.5s to 3s\n- Paradox: Paradoxical Swap projectile size reduced by 10%\n\n- Pocket: Barrage now respects line of sight\n- Pocket: Barrage amp effect now shows on Pocket\n- Pocket: Base Bullet Resist increased from -15% to 0%\n- Pocket: Base Spirit Resist reduced from 0% to -15%\n- Pocket: Barrage Amp per stack reduced from 10% to 8%\n- Pocket: Barrage T3 changed to +5% Amp Per Stack\n- Pocket: Fixed the camera moving too slowly after teleporting with Cloak\n\n- Seven: Fixed multiple instances of Static Charge not working correctly\n- Seven: Lightning Ball duration increased from 4s to 5s\n- Seven: Static Charge stun delay reduced from 4s to 3.5s\n\n- Vindicta: Stake base duration reduced from 2.5s to 2.25s\n- Vindicta: Stake T1 duration reduced from 1s to 0.75s\n\n- Viscous: Fixed Splatter direct hits counting as the second hit\n- Viscous: Fix Damage prevention after being hit by Viscous' Goo Ball being reduced by Debuff Reducer\n- Viscous: The Cube cast range increased from 25m to 30m\n- Viscous: The Cube T2 cooldown reduced from -25s to -22s\n- Viscous: Puddle Punch now deals light melee damage and is affected by melee items\n- Viscous: Puddle Punch Charge Delay increased from 0.5s to 1.5s\n- Viscous: Goo Ball duration reduced from 12s to 10s\n- Viscous: Goo Ball T1 duration reduced from +6s to +5s\n- Viscous: Goo Ball cooldown increased from 80s to 90s\n- Viscous: Goo Ball now deals 50% damage to bosses\n- Viscous: Goo Ball spirit resist reduced from 80% to 70%\n- Viscous: Goo Ball bullet resist reduced from 70% to 60%\n- Viscous: Goo Ball can now only stun and damage a target once every 1.5s, up from once every 1.0s\n- Viscous: Goo Ball acceleration lowered a bit\n- Viscous: Stunning or immobilizing Viscous now stops the ball from moving, and prevents him from doing damage and stunning\n\n- Warden: Binding Word no longer drains 1 stamina\n- Warden: Alchemical Flask cooldown reduced from 16s to 13s\n- Warden: Alchemical Flask T2 changed from -10s Cooldown to Drains 1 Stamina\n- Warden: Alchemical Flask T3 now also reduces cooldown by -7s\n- Warden: Binding Word immobilize duration reduced from 2s to 1.75\n- Warden: Binding Word now has a quick cast behavior (same as Static Charge)\n\n- Wraith: Card Trick now has 0.5s cd per cast\n- Wraith: Telekinesis T2 duration reduced from +0.75s to +0.5s\n- Wraith: Full Auto T2 changed to Imbues Bullets with +5 Spirit Damage (only affects Wraith)\n- Wraith: Fixed being able to melee and parry while stunned by Wraith's Telekinesis\n\n- Yamato: Base melee damage increased by 10%\n- Yamato: Flying Strike T3 now procs after the ability ends, rather than when connecting to the   "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added a new Profile page that displays a breakdown of recent matches and all-time player and hero statistics (Note: the newly added stats 'Souls Collected' and 'Healing' are starting at 0 for all players)\n\n- Added Report button to ESC menu player list to allow reporting of teammates in-game\n- Improved visibility of report button on post-game screen\n\n- You can now drag-and-drop items between categories in Builds\n- Added search to the Public Builds list, you can search by keyword within build names\n- Newly published hero builds now store the language of the build\n- Added 'Show All Languages' checkbox in Public Builds list, can be unchecked to only see builds in your language\n- Fixed issue with builds list not updating when changing between heroes in sandbox\n\n- Added localization support for German, Polish, and Korean.\n\n- Matchmaking window is now open 1 hour earlier on weekdays and weekends\n\n- Players can only pause once per game\n- A team can only pause at most 3 times in total amongst all the players\n- The game application now flashes when the game is unpaused\n\n- Various client and server performance improvements\n\n- Added streamer setting to the options panel. Causes player names in the game UI panels to be hidden.\n\n- Added a Melee Trainer bot in the 'gym' area of hero sandbox\n\n- Added new \"Game out of Date\" status message at the top of the hud, so you can quickly know when there is an update rather than finding out at queue time\n- Added a checkbox on the post game survey to make it not show again in the future\n- Items now show their spirit power impact when using alt\n- Fixed profile match history not remembering the history page you were on\n- Added Heal Amp and Debuff Resist to the Vitality stats in the shop\n- Hero details on the dashboard now show spirit information with alt, rather than requiring you to be in-game (this was added in a hotfix patch recently)\n- The game will now prevent any quick ability upgrades for 2 seconds after death, to help prevent against accidental upgrades\n- Can now reliably spectate a friend's game via the Friendlist\n- Miscellaneous fixes to friends and party menus\n- Added unavailable state to Zipline UI when zipline is on damage cooldown\n- Spectating controls now use Left/Right mouse buttons to switch between players on a team and space to switch teams. This now works with free cursor mode, and you can still click on portraits/minimap to spectate individual heroes.\n- Added new respawn countdown music 5 seconds before respawn\n- Added alternate shop music which plays in the neutral shop\n- Spectator count is now shown in-game\n- Player names are now shown to spectators\n- Added object motion blur\n- Optimized performance of distance field ambient occlusion\n\n- Fixed selling an item not removing charges correctly. Now you can't buy Extra Charge and immediately sell it and keep that readied charge.\n- Fixed some tooltips not showing golden statue bonuses if it wasn't the direct stat (i.e. you got fire rate but bullets per second didn't show the golden statue)\n- Fixed Sharpshooter showing up in stats for bullet velocity even though it doesn't increase your bullet velocity\n- Fixed a bug that caused items in the build page to not be dimmed when the shop was out of range\n- Fixed bug with Lash not hearing his own Ground Strike impact explosion sound\n- Added new effects for Withering Whip\n- Updated you change heroes in the sandbox\n- Fixed Zipline speed from Base Guardian killing and Zip booster ability not stacking properly\n- Fixed Base Guardian boost not properly doing its ramp up and instead instantly being fast\n- Added custom effects for Abram's Seismic Impact T3 buff\n- Fixed a bug where someone could be holding down M1 and still be attacking when the magic carpet arrives\n- Updated Lash Grapple cast sound\n- Added Decay impact sound\n- Removed wind and city ambient looping sounds\n- Volume and playback tweaks to one shot ambient sounds\n- Removed vent sounds in places where vents had been removed\n- Fixed some spectator music bugs\n- Updated negative feedback sound for clarity\n- Added hit confirm audio functionality to Warden's Alchemical Flask\n- Updated Majestic Leap sound\n- Updated bounce pad sound\n- Improved some Binding Word effects to be less noisy\n- Increased charged melee volume and falloff for victims\n- Reduced likelihood of irrelevant announcer and their teammates alone in a lane\n- Fixed a bunch of minor typos / grammar errors in various texts\n- Added 'Express' state to zipline indicators when speed had increased from defeating Enemy Base   },
````
````json
  "id": "2024-09-12",
  "posted_at": "2024-09-12",
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Matchmaking is now open 24/7 for all regions\n\n- Added new minimap\nUpdated base asset and added new unique Hero Icons​\nNeutral Camps are now represented by a highlight glow on the buildings they are located within​\nThe Orange Lane (Orchard) is now the Green Lane (Greenwich)​\n\n- Added a new behavior system. Players can temporarily lose access to certain capabilities in the game. The controls include Matchmaking, Voice Chat, Text Chat, Pausing, and Reporting. The system for tracking is active now, however we will wait on most penalties for a few days as we review the results.\n- There is now a low priority queue. The primary way to enter this is through abandoning games, but it can include other behavior. You must complete the required amount of games (escalates based on low priority frequency) to be able to return to using matchmaking normally.\n- Regularly misreporting players will result in you losing the reporting function and may in some cases cause you to go into low priority as well.\n\n- New players that are queued solo will only match with other new players until they get 4 wins\n- The lane allocation of 1-1-2-2- and 2-2-1-1 will no longer be a possible setup\n- A 6 player party will no longer match against solo players. This may result in longer queue times for 6 stacks.\n- AFK players will be automatically kicked out after a few minutes\n- Abandon Match dialog is now more clear about consequences of leaving\n- Abandon Match now has a time delay before you can click it to help protect against some accidental cases\n\n- Added custom hotkeys per hero\n\n- Bounce Pad visuals have been replaced with a wind fan (no mechanic changes). The effects also indicate the travel path.\n- Added little spirit frog legs to the Soul Urn\n- Orange zipline is now green\n\n- Space Bar no longer cancels abilities. Abilities can be pressed again to cancel the ones that have a cancel state.\n- The Alternate Cast modes \"Modifier Button\" and \"Alternate Cast Button\" have had their behaviors merged\n- Added UI button hints when using \"Alternate Cast Button\" in some extra cases\n- Added a slider for Double Tap Alternate Cast timing\n- Added a slider for Press and Hold Alternate Cast required time\n\n-Changes to Scoreboard (TAB) and Extra Info (ALT)\nPreviously there was a lot of crossover between the TAB and ALT modes during gameplay. In this update we have separated the behavior of these two modes to better serve different functions. The keys used for each of these modes can be swapped within Keybinds Settings.​\n​\nScoreboard (TAB):​\n- Enables cursor mode​\n- Displays Scoreboard​\n- Displays Damage Summary​\n- Allows minimap ping and draw​\n​\nExtra Info (ALT):​\n- No longer enables cursor mode​\n- Quick-upgrade Abilities (ie. ALT+1) each of these can be individually remapped​\n- Can now more easily map ALT as an Alternate Cast button​\n- Displays extra information on HUD (ie. Zipline Speed Boost Cooldown) - we plan to expand upon this in future updates​\n​\n\n- Client performance improvements\n- Game now automatically starts preloading the map once the client is launched, to help reduce load time for most users\n\n- Sand Box orb practice area now displays the time in milliseconds that you hit the orb after spawning\n\n- Added a console command (private_lobby_create) to allow players to create custom lobbies using our servers. This has very temp UI. Once we add a real UI to it along with some misc features, we'll add a non-console way to host custom lobbies.\n\n- Upgrade ability keys are now customizable (Current defaults are ALT+1, ALT+2, etc)\n- Adjusted Midboss low health roar sound to be more distinct from spawn sound\n- Added Maximum FPS Sliders for Dashboard and In-Game\n- Removed Pause limit for hero testing, coop bot matches, and the tutorial\n- Added support for launching the game in a secondary monitor properly\n- Fixed friends menu scrolling up automatically when it shouldn't\n- Fixed being unable to invite offline users to a party\n- Fixed invite tooltips on friends menu not being positioned correctly\n- Metal Skin effects revisions\n- Seven's Static Charge effects improvement to make the boundary more clear\n- Updated medic healing beam colors to be consistent with other heals\n- Melee troopers no longer play swipe effect (to reduce some unnecessary noise)\n- Added better indication when all four active slots are full\n- Updated Ice Dome to fix some performance related issues\n- Updated Torment visual effects to reduce some noise\n- Added sound for Tesla Bullets proc\n- Added sound for Toxic Bullets proc\n- Divine Barrier wings no longer fully opaque\n- Reduced intensity of last hit status effect\n- Updated Echo Shard sound effects\n- Added low ammo indication sound\n- Improved clarity for Seven casting and channeling sounds\n- Updated Refresher effects colors from green to purple\n- Minor updates to Abram's Seismic Impact to resolve some states faster to reduce visual noise\n- Updated Lady Geist Blood Bomb, Life Drain, Malice, and Soul Exchange sounds\n- Added Shoulder Charge wall impact effects\n- Changed open mic audio settings to allow open mic to opt in to team chat or party chat\n- Updated effects for the Warp Stone bullet resist buff\n- Added Lucky Shot proc sound effect\n- Updated Berserker buff effects\n- Adjusted Shiv's running reload animation\n- Added wall impact animation for Abram's Shoulder Charge\n- Lowered gun arm during Abram's jump animation to not obscure reticle\n- Soul Jar return location effects will change color based on the captured state of the Soul Jar\n- Lady Geist Blood bomb revisions to improve visibility in team fights\n- Improved visibility of heroes caught in the Singularity\n- Distance field shadows and AO no longer visible behind cosmic veils\n- Added sliders for FSR and FSR2 sharpness\n- Updated Wraith, Grey Talon, and Infernus VO\n- Walker AoE Stomp has new effects\n- Viscous Splatter effects have been adjusted\n- Revised Silencer cast and duration sounds\n- Added Rebirth channel and revive sounds\n- Added Infuser cast sound\n- Audio bug fixes related to switching spectating targets\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Orbs now favor the claimer rather than the denier when both impact within a very narrow window of each other\n\n- Flex Slot moved from \"First Walker\" to \"Two Walkers\"\n- All ability and item cooldowns increased by 6% globally\n- Max respawn time now happens at 35 minutes rather than 40 minutes\n- T4 Items Cost reduced from 6300 to 6200\n- Shield regeneration time reduced from 60s to 50s\n\n- Wall Jump activation has been made more lenient\n- Wall Jump launch angle can now be steered a bit more\n- Jump shooting lockout period reduced from 0.25s to 0.15s\n- Souls from bosses now appear much more spread out\n- Rope climbing speed increased from 13 to 14\n- Taking non-dot damage while on a rope slows you by 30%\n- Improved Heavy melee hit registration\n- Mid Boss warning now happens at 60% instead of 50%\n- Walker beam max range (the sticky portion after being acquired) reduced by 20% (50m to 40m)\n- Successful Parry CD reduced from 0.5s to 0.25s\n\n- Fixed Alt Fires not working with conditional bonuses (like Close Range)\n- Melee trooper hitbox improved\n- Fixed Medic Troopers sometimes using their heal on Guardian boss\n- Fixed multiple heroes getting the power-up bonus by standing on top of it before spawns\n- Fixed your gun reloading down to a smaller magazine when reloading with higher ammo than your max\n\n\n[ Weapon Items ]\n\n- Headshot Booster: Bonus damage reduced from 45 to 40\n- High-Velocity Mag: Weapon Damage reduced from 14% to 12%\n- Restorative Shot: Healing from heroes increased from 35 to 40\n- Restorative Shot: Healing from troopers increased from 10 to 15\n- Hollow Point Round: Conditional Weapon Damage increased from 20% to 22%\n- Rapid Rounds: Fire Rate reduced from 12% to 9%\n- Rapid Rounds: No longer provides -50 Max Health\n\n- Active Reload: Lifesteal reduced from 22% to 20%\n- Mystic Shot: Spirit damage now considers bullet falloff\n- Mystic Shot: Cooldown reduced from 6s to 5s\n- Berserker: Bullet Resist reduced from 8% to 7%\n- Long Range: Bullet Shield increased from +90 to +140\n- Melee Charge: Charge distance bonus is moved to innate section\n- Melee Charge: Passive now has a 8s cooldown\n- Melee Charge: When passive triggers, you deal bonus +20% Melee Damage\n- Melee Charge: Now gives a UI indication when reload procs\n- Soul Shredder Bullets: Weapon Damage increased from 6% to 7%\n- Fleetfoot: Active move speed reduced from 4 to 3\n- Fleetfoot: Cooldown reduced from 22s to 18s\n- Fleetfoot: Health increased from +75 to +90\n\n- Warpstone: Range reduced from 13m to 11m\n- Heroic Aura: Active movement speed bonus reduced from 3 to 2\n- Burst Fire: Now provides +70 Health\n- Sharpshooter: Bullet Health increased from +175 to +200\n- Hunter's Aura: Bullet Resist reduction reduced from -10% to -9%\n- Titanic Magazine: Bullet Resist increased from 15% to 18%\n- Alchemical Fire: Spirit Power increased from +7 to +11\n\n- Crippling Headshot: Debuff duration reduced from 5s to 4s\n- Lucky Shot: Bonus Damage increased from 80% to 90%\n- Glass Cannon: Max stacks increased from 5 to 7\n- Silencer: Damage increased from 15% to 20%\n- Frenzy: Linger duration increased from 4s to 6s\n\n\n[ Vitality Items ]\n\n- Healing Rite: Now grants +3 Spirit Power\n- Enduring Spirit: Spirit Lifesteal increased from 8% to 10%\n- Melee Lifesteal: Melee damage reduced from 15% to 13%\n\n- Health Nova: Now requires Healing Rite\n- Health Nova: Now grants +4 Spirit Power\n- Health Nova: Range reduced from 18m to 15m\n- Divine Barrier: Bullet Shield shield reduced from 300 to 260\n- Divine Barrier: 10% of Max HP to 9%\n- Improved Reach: Spirit Power increased from +6 to +8\n- Torment Pulse: Health increased from +125 to +140\n- Silence Glyph: Spirit Shield reduced from +200 to +150\n\n- Echo   "raw_content": "- Replaced the recent soul duplication hotfix with new behavior. Pre 10 min, lanes now always split orbs when there are more heroes than the assigned participants for that lane (3+ people in a dual lane, 2+ people in a solo). Previously it only split with 3+ people regardless of the lane, which is what allowed soul abuses when dipping into a solo lane.\n- Private lobbies can now assign players to duo and solo lanes\n- Fixed some recent bugs with Vindicta Flight that could cause some large bursts in a direction\n- Fixed being able to shoot while using Ethereal Shift and flying with Vindicta\n- Veil Walker: Fire Rate reduced from 30% to 20%",
````
````json
  "title": "09-19-2024 Update",
  "url": "https://forums.playdeadlock.com/threads/09-19-2024-update.31166/"
}
````

````json
  "raw_content": "[ General Changes ]\n\n- Added new hero, Mirage.\n\n\n\n\n- Added an initial Anti-Cheat detection system. When a user is detected as cheating, during the game session the opponents will be given a choice between banning the user immediately and ending the match or turning the cheater into a frog for the rest of the game and then banning them afterwards. The system is set to conservative detection levels as we work on a v2 anti-cheat system that is more extensive. We will turn on the banning of users in a couple of days after the update is out. When a match is ended this way, the results will not count for other players.\n\n\n\n- Added global quickcast support. You can now configure your abilities and/or items to be on quickcast mode globally. You can also customize each ability individually to be quickcast or not. This also supports grenade style abilities.\n- Hero specific keybinds now say the ability name instead of ability 1-4\n- Added Zipline hotkey that can be independently bound from space/jump\n- Fixed per-hero keys not inheriting user-defined default keys properly\n- Added keybind indicator for hero-specific binds showing which keys have been bound for that hero\n\n- Added Custom Match play mode\n- Private lobbies now have an option to turn on cheats\n- Private lobbies now let you select the specific servers to use\n- Private lobbies now have an option to make the match publicly visible or private to only the players\n- Private lobbies now support post-game and replays properly\n- Private lobbies now allow picking specific lanes (solo and dual lane colors are displayed)\n\n- Added a cheat report option in-game and post-game\n- Can now mute and report enemy players using ESC while in-game\n\n- Updated the game access invite UI to make it clear when users have been accepted (in most cases we look into where a user said their friend wasn't accepted, the user was actually accepted but they never claimed the game on the Steam notifications side)\n\n- Added voice indicators for when players are speaking in party and team chat (green for party, white for team)\n- Updated Player Card and action menu when clicking on a player in the friend list or ESC menu\n- Fixed Rich Presence for Steam and in-game friend lists\n- Party Code is now hidden when you enable Streamer Mode\n\n- Added Region indicator to the matches on the watch tab\n- Fixed search by MatchID not working for games that are live\n- Added Spectator Fog of War toggle (hotkeys available in settings)\n\n- Added South Africa servers\n- Added Italian localization\n\n- Instead of showing the total number of favorites for each build, we now tag popular builds using a mix of both recent and historical usage so that newer builds are easier to find\n- Builds that haven't been updated in 30 days now have a distinct visual\n\n- General rendering performance improvements\n- Shadow quality improvements for higher shadow settings\n- Fixed issues with player outline rendering in FSR2\n- Fixed regression with saturation volumes outline not showing up\n- Displacement mapping no longer an official option and will likely be cut (it can be enabled with a ConVar after the game starts)\n- Lighting improvements in map\n\n- Picking up a powerup will now list all the stats they modify in the HUD instead of a generic name like 'Casting' or 'Gun'\n- Spirit scaling is now always shown on the tooltips without needing to hold alt/tab\n- Updated Viscous icon to look more different from Dynamo\n- Added frosted shell effect on the rejuv crystal when Frozen Shelter is active\n- Music and Sound effects will now pause during game pause\n- Fixed Guardian trying to shoot people unsuccessfully behind cover\n\n- Fixed Quicksilver Reload bonus damage not working correctly with things like Scorn and Flog\n- Added timer sound to the last couple seconds of Flying Cloak\n- Wall Jumps now use better animations to communicate directionality\n- Fixed jumping between ziplines consuming stamina\n- Fixed Magic Reverb not getting increased bonus damage from damage amps\n- Improved preloading during queue time\n- Updated Killing Blow effects\n- Updated Lightning Ball effects to reduce some visual noise\n- Fixed some issues with Crimson Slash effect not appearing reliably\n- Viscous Puddle Punch will now show a red preview for when the punch is out of range but within 2x of the cast range\n- Fixed Ricochet not respecting LOS when picking the targets\n- No longer play low ammo sounds when firing while sliding\n- Fixed not being able to bind Reload to Mouse Wheel Up/Down\n- Minimap line colors now match the color of the hero drawing them\n- Fixed spectator keybinds not showing correctly\n- Updated Flying Strike effects to reduce some visual noise\n- Fixed Flex Slot unlock order not matching the usual order of unlocks\n- Updated Sleep Dagger projectile, impact and sleeping debuff visuals\n- Fixed imbue and active abilities dialog not responding to keybinds if your ability/item binds included a modifier key or were bound to the mouse wheel\n- Fixed Dynamo not playing an animation during the cast delay on Singularity\n- Updated bullet shield break effects\n- Updated Unstoppable effects to be clearer at cast time\n- Warp Stone: Casting while on the ground will no longer stop the player in place, will try to move forward along the ground instead\n- Warp Stone: Fixed cases of Warp Stone getting caught on geometry\n- Fixed various projectiles going through bosses (Wraith cards, Geist bombs, etc)\n- Fixed Dash not always breaking breakables\n- Breakables will now wait to spawn if a player is in the way\n- Abrams now has a new custom heavy melee animation\n- Updated Haze VO\n- Fixed a bug where Kelvin desperation VO for Arctic Beam and Ice Path were flipped\n- Turned off a Shiv line where he referenced slowing down enemies when he wasn't actually doing so\n- Removed reason 31 of why Lash is better than Bebop and replaced it with reason 26 of why Lash is better than Bebop (fixed a bug with Lash's hero select lines)\n- Replaced a Grey Talon select line to make his intentions more clear\n- Infernus will no longer appear in every bot match\n- Bots can now choose to play Lady Geist, Mirage, Shiv, and Warden\n- Fixed min/max falloff range displaying incorrectly on Weapon Stats Tooltip\n- Updated Rejuvenator buff effect\n- Updated wall bounce effects for Viscous in ball form\n- Added citadel_give_gold cheat command\n- Improved read on the connecting rope for Flying Strike and reduced visual obstruction of the ending slash\n- Music bug fixes for replay and spectating\n- Allow announcer lines for kill streaks and various hero lines to play while the player is in an active combat encounter\n- Timing critical music and sfx will pause and resume in response to game pause\n\n\n\n[ Misc Gameplay ]\n\n- Side lanes are now a little further apart from the middle lanes\n- Added a First Blood bonus bounty for the first kill each player gets. Grants 150 bonus souls.\n- Lane setup is now always 1-2-2-1\n- Zipline Boost now starts on cooldown again\n- Trooper damage reduction from Guardians reduced from 35% to 28% (they die a little faster to Guardians now)\n- Trooper share radius reduced (from allied hero to allied hero 50m->40m, from orb to allied hero 40m->35m)\n- Reverted recent creep sharing duplication changes (back to how it was over a week ago, we want to see if it's still an issue with the other changes in this patch)\n\n- Some latency-related calculation improvements that help orbs be a little less deny favored\n- Orbs now appear a little smaller to the enemy that is trying to deny them\n\n- Added a Soul Generator (like the one in hero sandbox) in the respawn area. Starts spawning orbs at 3 minutes. Each orb is worth 10 souls when shot. (primary purpose for this is cases when you are very close to an item purchase)\n\n- Fire Rate slows now stack diminishingly\n\n- Rejuv bonus creep HP increased from 50% to 70%\n- Rejuv bonus is no longer consumed for dead players, they have it on their next life now\n\n- Troopers no longer give half bounty when base guardians are destroyed for that lane\n- Killer to Assist bounty ratio reduced from 2.0 to 1.7 (slightly more to assists)\n- Midgame respawn time increased a bit (reaches 50s at 20 min instead of 40s at 20 min)\n- Teleporter delay reduced from 5s to 4s\n- Guardians now deal 10% more damage to players\n\n- Sinner's Sacrifice now grants a permanent golden statue bonus when it is killed\n- Fixed Sinner's Sacrifice sharing bounty to nearby allies\n\n- Added indicator on the hud to help track your passive cooldown items when they are 1s from ready (headshot Booster, Medic Bullets, etc)\n\n- Boon count increased from 11 to 14 (added to 16/18/20k)\n- Non-Health boon bonuses rescaled over the 14 levels (same total as before)\n\n- Aggressive crouch spamming within a very narrow window will now cause you to very briefly move progressively slower\n- Added Rejuv drop on a loop in the sandbox mode\n- Added infinite ammo option to sandbox (\"No Reload\")\n- Fixed dashing downwards not destroying breakables\n- Silence now deselects your ability if you had it open when silenced\n\n\n\n[ Weapon Items ]\n\n- Monster Rounds: Bullet Resist vs NPCs reduced from 35% to 30%\n- High-Velocity Mag: Bullet Velocity reduced from +30% to +25%\n- Restorative Shot: Cooldown reduced from 6.2s to 5.5s\n- Hollow Point Ward: Spirit Shield increased from +85 to +95\n- Headshot Booster: Fire Rate reduced from +5% to +4%\n\n- Kinetic Dash: Fire Rate reduced from 25% to 20%\n- Kinetic Dash: Fire Rate max duration reduced from 8s to 7s\n- Berserker: Damage required per stack reduced from 110 to 100\n- Mystic Shot: Damage spirit scaling increased from 0.6 to 0.8\n- Melee Charge: Impact now increases your ammo for that mag instead of reloading (so if your ammo is 2/8, it becomes 10/8). Reloads you instantly if in the middle of a reload.\n- Melee Charge: Cooldown increased from 8.5s to 10s\n\n- Intensifying Magazine: Max Weapon Damage reduced from 75% to 60%\n- Escalating Resilience: Fire Rate reduced from 14% to 12%\n- Headhunter: Now requires Headshot Booster\n- Headhunter: No longer grants +50% Bullet Velocity\n- Headhunter: Now grants +5% Fire Rate\n- Headhunter: Fixed not being affected by cooldown reduction\n- Pristine Emblem: Now requires High-Velocity Mag\n- Pristine Emblem: Now grants +35% Bullet Velocity\n- Titanic Magazine: Ammo increased from 100% to 120%\n- Sharpshooter: Fixed falloff reduction not working properly\n\n- Frenzy: Low HP Spirit Resist changed to Bullet Resist\n- Spiritual Overflow: Now grants +250 Bullet Shield\n- Silencer: Now grants +12 Spirit\n- Ricochet: Fire Rate reduced from 12% to 10%\n- Vampiric Burst: Fixed casting it interrupting sliding\n- Siphon Bullets: Moved to T4 Vitality\n- Shadow Weave: Now a T4 Weapon Item\n\n\n\n[ Vitality Items ]\n\n- Melee Lifesteal: Melee Damage reduced from 13% to 12%\n- Melee Lifesteal: Heal vs non-heroes reduced from 40% to 30%\n- Extra Stamina: Stamina Recovery increased from 10% to 14%\n\n- Restorative Locket: No longer requires max stacks to restore a stamina point\n- Divine Barrier: Bonus Health reduced from 75 to 50\n- Combat Barrier: Fire Rate while shielded reduced from 8% to 6%\n- Combat Barrier: Weapon Damage while shielded reduced from 28% to 25%\n- Combat Barrier: Bullet Shield increased from +300 to +325\n- Health Nova: Weapon Damage increased from 10% to 12%\n- Return Fire: While active grants +25% Bullet Resistance\n- Return Fire: Bullet Damage Returned reduced from 70% to 60%\n- Return Fire: No longer grants +7% Fire Rate\n- Return Fire: Spirit Power increased from +7 to +9\n- Return Fire: Fixed Ricochet'd bullets not returning the right amount of damage\n- Bullet Armor: Bullet Resist increased from 20% to 25%\n\n- Veil Walker: No longer grants +20% Fire Rate\n- Improved Bullet Armor: Bullet Resist increased from 45% to 50%\n- Fortitude: Bonus Health increased from 275 to 300\n- Lifestrike: Cooldown reduced from 5.25s to 5s\n- Lifestrike: Fixed proccing heal more than once when hitting multiple targets\n\n- Shadow Weave: Moved to T4 Weapon\n- Siphon Bullets: Now a T4 Vitality Item\n- Siphon Bullets: No longer grants +28% Weapon Damage\n- Siphon Bullets: Now grants +18% Bullet Resistance\n- Colossus: Slow radius increased from 12m to 14m\n- Soul Rebirth: Increases base respawn rate by +15s\n- Unstoppable: Can no longer be cast during channels\n\n\n\n[ Spirit Items ]\n\n- Ammo Scavenger: Ammo reduced from 15% to 10%\n- Ammo Scavenger: Health reduced from 60 to 40\n- Ammo Scavenger: Duration reduced from 35s to 30s\n- Ammo Scavenger: Max stacks reduced from 12 to 10\n- Spirit Strike: No longer grants +0.8 Health Regen\n- Mystic Reach: Ability Range increased from 16% to 18%\n- Mystic Reach: Now provides 6% Bullet Resist instead of 6% Spirit Resist\n\n- Withering Whip: No longer grants +8% Fire Rate\n- Withering Whip: Spirit Power increased from +4 to +6\n- Withering Whip: Now grants +1 Sprint\n- Withering Whip: Cast range increased from 24m to 30m\n- Withering Whip: Fire Rate slow increased from 40% to 45%\n- Quicksilver Reload: No longer triggers if your ammo is at max value already\n- Decay: Cooldown increased from 32s to 45s\n- Decay: Cast range scaling reduced from 0.2 to 0.16\n\n- Improved Reach: Now provides 12% Bullet Resist instead of 12% Spirit Resist\n- Improved Spirit: Spirit Power increased from +21 to +23\n- Knockdown: Now causes enemies to fall down faster\n- Torment Pulse: Health Bonus increased from 140 to 160\n- Torment Pulse: Spirit Power damage scaling increased from 0.25 to 0.33\n- Rapid Recharge: Faster Time Between Charges increased from +55% to +65%\n- Rapid Recharge: Cooldown Reduction For Charged Abilities increased from +25% to 30%\n\n- Magic Carpet: Fixed it consuming stamina when jumping off\n- Escalating Exposure: Fixed the bonus damage being reduced twice by resistance\n- Refresher: Bullet Resist increased from 8% to 16%\n- Refresher: Spirit Resist reduced from 16% to 8%\n- Echo Shard: Now has a 0.3s cast delay (like Refresher)\n\n\n\n[ Hero Changes ]\n\n- Abrams: Infernal Resilience reduced from 16% to 15%\n- Abrams: Infernal Resilience T3 reduced from 9% to 8%\n- Abrams: Siphon Life spirit scaling increased from 0.2 to 0.3\n- Abrams: Fixed Cases where Shoulder Charge was unexpectedly 'slamming' in to walls and stairs\n\n- Bebop: Hook range reduced from 30m to 25m\n- Bebop: Hyper Beam duration spirit scaling reduced from 0.08 to 0.06\n\n- Dynamo: Singularity radius reduced from 8m to 7m\n\n- Grey Talon: Arrow cycle time reduced from 0.4s to 0.45s (overall dps unchanged)\n- Grey Talon: Rain of Arrows can be alternate-casted to remain near the ground\n- Grey Talon: Fixed air dash during Rain of Arrows going half the distance\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl bonus spirit on death now has a 3s buffer window\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl: very slight turn rate improvements\n- Grey Talon: Guided Owl radius increased from 12m to 13m\n\n- Haze: Sleep dagger hitbox reduced by 10%\n- Haze: Fixed refresher not working properly with Smoke Bomb\n- Haze: Fixed some items that didn't proc before: Tesla Bullets, Lucky Shot, Mystic Shot\n- Haze: Bullet Dance now more accurately shows who it is shooting\n- Haze: Fixed Bullet Dance sometimes not obeying line of sight accurately\n- Haze: Bullet Dance bullets are now affected by Time Wall\n- Haze: Bullet Dance evasion reduced from 50% to 25%\n- Haze: Bullet Dance fire rate reduced from 25% to 15%\n\n- Ivy: Stone Form now works against airborne targets\n- Ivy: Kudzu Bomb T3 now also grants +2m Radius\n- Ivy: Kudzu Bomb spirit power scaling increased from 0.6 to 0.7\n- Ivy: Fixed clicking noise when flying while disarmed\n- Ivy: Fixed being able to Stone Form during the Take Flight animation to circumvent the vulnerable cast period\n\n- Kelvin: Headshot bonus damage reduced by 20%\n- Kelvin: Bullet damage growth reduced from 1.2 to 0.9\n- Kelvin: Arctic Beam movement slow reduced from 70% to 50%\n- Kelvin: Moving through breakables while on Ice Path now destroys them\n\n- Lady Geist: Life Drain cooldown reduced from 42s to 30s\n- Lady Geist: Malice cooldown reduced from 6.25s to 6s\n- Lady Geist: Malice T1 improved from -2.75s Cooldown to -3s\n- Lady Geist: Fixed Life Drain not healing extra based on amplifications (like Soul Shredder and Malice stacks)\n- Lady Geist: Sprint increased from 1 to 1.5\n\n- McGinnis: Fixed looking up circumventing the min range on Wall and Barrage\n- McGinnis: Can now cast parry to cancel your ult\n- McGinnis: Fixed various issues with Spectral Wall indoors and near walls spawning the wrong number of segments\n\n- Mo & Krill: Combo duration reduced from 2.75s to 2.5s\n- Mo & Krill: Combo damage spirit scaling increased from 0.8 to 1.1\n- Mo & Krill: Combo kill trigger now has a 3s buffer window to get credit\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast T1 reduced from +1.5s to +1s\n- Mo & Krill: Sand Blast range increased from 30m to 35m\n- Mo & Krill: Burrow base speed increased from 3 to 4\n- Mo & Krill: Burrow T3 speed reduced from +3 to +2\n\n- Paradox: Pulse Grenade T3 now also grants +2% Damage Amp\n- Paradox: Fixed being unable to be damaged by multiple Time Walls\n\n- Pocket: Affliction no longer goes through walls, now respects line of sight\n- Pocket: Barrage T3 reduced from +5% to +4%\n\n- Seven: Static Charge stun duration reduced from 1.1 to 0.9\n- Seven: Static Charge T3 reduced from 1.1 to 0.9\n- Seven: Static Charge radius reduced from 6m to 5m\n- Seven: Static Charge can now be   "posted_at": "2025-10-02",
````
````json
  "raw_content": "[ General ]\n\n* Weapon Investment bonus increased from 7/9/13/20/29/40/58/72/83/93% to 7/9/13/20/29/40/60/75/95/115 (for 0.8/1.6/2.4/3.2/4.8/7.2/9.6/16/22.4/28.8k spent)\n\n* Applying a CC to an enemy is reduced in duration when affected by other CCs in a short interval. Each previously applied CC reduces the duration by 7% up to a max of 21% over the past 7 seconds. CC is defined as the same set of debuffs that affect Reactive Barrier (stun, sleep, immobilize, tether, etc). Note that abilities that apply multiple things only count as one CC (aka Abrams shoulder charge CC on the grab and the stun, Lash ult grab and stun, etc).\n\n* Matchmaking updates to include more considerations for a balanced roster beyond only frontliners\n\n* Reduced shooting lock-out period when jumping from 0.15s to 0.0s\n* Shooting while jumping now applies a -10% inaccuracy period for 0.15s after jumping\n\n* Wall Jumps after the first one now cost 0.5 stamina (first wall jump per sequence is free)\n* Reduced corner boosting velocity by 20%\n\n* Mid Boss base HP increased from 12500 to 13000\n* Mid Boss damage resistance per second reduced from 50 + 7/min to 35 + 5/min\n* Rejuv bonus HP reduced from 15% to 10%\n* 2nd Midboss rejuv credits reduced from 4 to 3\n\n* Dropped Unsecured soul bags now require a Heavy Melee to secure rather than standing ontop of them\n\n* Soul Urn: Carriers now have +30% bullet and +30% spirit Resist\n* Soul Urn: Movespeed bonus reduced from +7m to +3.5m\n* Soul Urn: Damage taken when holding too long increased from 1% to 1.5% Max Health per second\n\n* Added an extra boon at 12k (nearby boon levels adjusted slightly, 10.2/11.6/13.2k to 10/11/12/13.2k)\n\n* Neutral Shop no longer gets disabled if an enemy is nearby\n* Distance to attack Walkers reduced from 35m to 34m\n* Fixed ordering issues with various items with Bullet based Procs. Debuffs (ie. negative Bullet Resist) from all procs are now applied before any bonus damage from the procs. Items include Weakening Headshot, Crippling Headshot, Headshot Booster, Headhunter, Hollow Point and Mystic Shot.\n* Enabled input buffering for shooting, dashing, melee and parry. This allows input to register before the current action is finished\n\n\n\n\n[ Heroes ]\n\n* Abrams: Siphon Life damage and spirit scaling reduced by 10%\n* Abrams: Siphon Life heal ratio increased by 10%\n* Abrams: Shoulder Charge T1 changed from +25% Weapon Power to +20 Bullet Damage Per Shot (results in a weaker followup melee hit)\n\n* Billy: Add footsteps to Billy's out of combat run animations\n* Billy: Base move speed reduced from 7.3 to 7.1\n* Billy: Blasted T1 movespeed increased from +1.75 to +2.25m\n\n* Calico: Base bullet damage increased from 1.9 to 2.0\n* Calico: Gloom
````
